
using System;
using System.Linq;
using CoffeeRuu.Data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Services
{
    public class AuthenticatedUser
    {
        public int EmployeeId { get; set; }
        public int RoleId { get; set; }
        public string Username { get; set; } = "";
        public string FullName { get; set; } = "";
        public string RoleName { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class AuthenticationService
    {
        private readonly Func<CoffeeRuuDbContext> _contextFactory;

        public AuthenticationService(
            Func<CoffeeRuuDbContext> contextFactory)
        {
            _contextFactory = contextFactory
                ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(contextFactory));
        }

        public AuthenticatedUser? Authenticate(
            string username,
            string password)
        {
            using var db = _contextFactory();

            var employee = db.Employees
                .FirstOrDefault(e => e.Username == username);

            if (employee == null ||
                !employee.IsActive ||
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(employee.PasswordHash) ||
                !PasswordService.VerifyPassword(
                    password,
                    employee.PasswordHash))
            {
                return null;
            }

            var role = db.Roles.FirstOrDefault(
                r => r.RoleId == employee.RoleId);

            if (role == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Không tìm thấy vai trò của tài khoản.");
            }

            return new AuthenticatedUser
            {
                EmployeeId = employee.EmployeeId,
                RoleId = employee.RoleId,
                Username = employee.Username,
                FullName = employee.FullName,
                RoleName = role.RoleName
            };
        }
    }
}

using System.Windows;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Services
{
    public interface IMessageDialogService
    {
        void ShowInfo(string title, string message);
        void ShowWarning(string title, string message);
        void ShowError(string title, string message);
        bool Confirm(string title, string message);
    }

    public class MessageDialogService : IMessageDialogService
    {
        public void ShowInfo(string title, string message)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Information);
        }

        public void ShowWarning(string title, string message)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Warning);
        }

        public void ShowError(string title, string message)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Error);
        }

        public bool Confirm(string title, string message)
        {
            return MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
                MessageBoxImage.Question) == MessageBoxResult.Yes;
        }
    }
}
using System.Windows;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Services
{
    public interface IMessageDialogService
    {
        void ShowInfo(string title, string message);
        void ShowWarning(string title, string message);
        void ShowError(string title, string message);
        bool Confirm(string title, string message);
    }

    public class MessageDialogService : IMessageDialogService
    {
        public void ShowInfo(string title, string message)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Information);
        }

        public void ShowWarning(string title, string message)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Warning);
        }

        public void ShowError(string title, string message)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Error);
        }

        public bool Confirm(string title, string message)
        {
            return MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
                MessageBoxImage.Question) == MessageBoxResult.Yes;
        }
    }
}



using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;

namespace CoffeeRuu
{
    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        public MainWindow()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            var authenticationService =
                new AuthenticationService(App.CreateDbContext);

            var dialogService = new MessageDialogService();

            var windowService = new WindowService(this);

            DataContext = new LoginViewModel(
                authenticationService,
                dialogService,
                windowService);

            PasswordInput.PasswordChanged += PasswordInput_PasswordChanged;
        }

        private void PasswordInput_PasswordChanged(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (DataContext is LoginViewModel viewModel)
            {
                viewModel.Password = PasswordInput.Password;
            }
        }

        private void Window_KeyDown(
            object sender,
            System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key == System.Windows.Input.Key.Enter)
            {
                if (DataContext is LoginViewModel viewModel
                    && viewModel.LoginCommand.CanExecute(null))
                {
                    viewModel.LoginCommand.Execute(null);
                }

                e.Handled = true;
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CoffeeRuu.Data;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Services
{
    public class RecipeService
    {
        private readonly Func<CoffeeRuuDbContext> _contextFactory;

        public RecipeService(Func<CoffeeRuuDbContext> contextFactory)
        {
            _contextFactory = contextFactory;
        }

        public async Task<List<Ingredient>> GetIngredientsAsync()
        {
            using var db = _contextFactory();

            return await db.Ingredients
                .AsNoTracking()
                .OrderBy(i => i.IngredientName)
                .ToListAsync();
        }

        public async Task<List<Product>> GetProductsAsync()
        {
            using var db = _contextFactory();

            return await db.Products
                .Include(p => p.Category)
                .AsNoTracking()
                .OrderBy(p => p.ProductName)
                .ToListAsync();
        }

        public async Task<List<ProductSize>> GetSizesAsync(int productId)
        {
            using var db = _contextFactory();

            return await db.ProductSizes
                .Where(s => s.ProductId == productId)
                .AsNoTracking()
                .OrderBy(s => s.ProductSizeId)
                .ToListAsync();
        }

        public async Task<List<ProductRecipe>> GetRecipesAsync(int productSizeId)
        {
            using var db = _contextFactory();

            return await db.ProductRecipes
                .Include(r => r.Ingredient)
                .Where(r => r.ProductSizeId == productSizeId)
                .AsNoTracking()
                .ToListAsync();
        }

        // Trả về null nếu sản phẩm có size nhưng người dùng chưa chọn size.
        public async Task<string?> SaveRecipeAsync(
            int productId,
            int? selectedSizeId,
            int ingredientId,
            decimal quantity)
        {
            using var db = _contextFactory();

            ProductSize? productSize = null;

            if (selectedSizeId.HasValue)
            {
                productSize = await db.ProductSizes
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(s =>
                        s.ProductSizeId == selectedSizeId.Value &&
                        s.ProductId == productId);

                if (productSize == null)
                {
                    throw new InvalidOperationException(
                        "Size được chọn không thuộc sản phẩm.");
                }
            }

            if (productSize == null)
            {
                bool hasSize = await db.ProductSizes
                    .AnyAsync(s => s.ProductId == productId);

                if (hasSize)
                {
                    return null;
                }

                productSize = new ProductSize
                {
                    ProductId = productId,
                    SizeName = "Standard",
                    Price = 0
                };

                db.ProductSizes.Add(productSize);
                await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            var existingRecipe = await db.ProductRecipes
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(r =>
                    r.ProductSizeId == productSize.ProductSizeId &&
                    r.IngredientId == ingredientId);

            string message;

            if (existingRecipe != null)
            {
                existingRecipe.QuantityUsed = quantity;
                message = "Cập nhật định lượng thành công!";
            }
            else
            {
                var newRecipe = new ProductRecipe
                {
                    ProductSizeId = productSize.ProductSizeId,
                    IngredientId = ingredientId,
                    QuantityUsed = quantity
                };

                db.ProductRecipes.Add(newRecipe);
                message = "Thêm nguyên liệu vào công thức thành công!";
            }

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            return message;
        }

        public async Task<bool> DeleteRecipeAsync(
            int productSizeId,
            int ingredientId)
        {
            using var db = _contextFactory();

            var recipe = await db.ProductRecipes.FindAsync(
                productSizeId,
                ingredientId);

            if (recipe == null)
            {
                return false;
            }

            db.ProductRecipes.Remove(recipe);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            return true;
        }
    }
}

using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;
using System;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views;

public partial class StockReceiptView : Page
{
    private readonly StockReceiptViewModel _viewModel;
    private bool _isLoaded;

    public StockReceiptView(
        StockReceiptViewModel viewModel)
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        _viewModel = viewModel
            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(viewModel));

        DataContext = _viewModel;
    }

    private async void Page_Loaded(
        object sender,
        RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (_isLoaded)
            return;

        _isLoaded = true;

        await _viewModel.LoadAsync();
    }
}

using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class ProductDialog : Window
    {
        private readonly ProductDialogViewModel _viewModel;

        // Trả kết quả sản phẩm cho MenuDialogService.
        public Product? ProductResult => _viewModel.ProductResult;

        public ProductDialog(
            MenuService menuService,
            Product? product = null)
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            // Khởi tạo ViewModel.
            _viewModel = new ProductDialogViewModel(
                menuService,
                product);

            DataContext = _viewModel;

            // Nhận yêu cầu đóng cửa sổ từ ViewModel.
            _viewModel.CloseRequested += result =>
            {
                DialogResult = result;
            };

            // Tải danh mục khi mở cửa sổ.
            Loaded += async (_, _) =>
            {
                await _viewModel.LoadCategoriesAsync();
            };
        }
    }
}

using CoffeeRuu.Repositories;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class PosMenuView : UserControl
    {
        private readonly PosMenuViewModel _viewModel;

        public PosMenuView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            var menuService = new MenuService(
                new MenuRepository(App.CreateDbContext()));

            var orderService = new OrderService(
                new OrderRepository(App.CreateDbContext));

            IPrintService printService = new PrintService();

            _viewModel = new PosMenuViewModel(
                menuService,
                orderService,
                printService);

            // Các hộp thoại là phần giao tiếp giữa View và ViewModel.
            _viewModel.ShowItemNoteDialog = ShowItemNoteDialog;
            _viewModel.ShowQrPaymentDialog = ShowQrPaymentDialog;
            _viewModel.ShowMessage = ShowMessage;
            _viewModel.NavigateToKds = NavigateToKds;

            DataContext = _viewModel;

            Loaded += PosMenuView_Loaded;
        }

        private void PosMenuView_Loaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _viewModel.LoadDataCommand.Execute(null);
        }

        // Hộp thoại ghi chú món.
        private string? ShowItemNoteDialog(
            string productName,
            string sizeName)
        {
            var dialog = new Window
            {
                Title = "Ghi chú món",
                Width = 420,
                Height = 280,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                Owner = Window.GetWindow(this),
                ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize,
                Background = Brushes.White
            };

            var panel = new StackPanel
            {
                Margin = new Thickness(20)
            };

            panel.Children.Add(new TextBlock
            {
                Text = $"{productName} - {sizeName}",
                FontSize = 18,
                FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 15)
            });

            panel.Children.Add(new TextBlock
            {
                Text = "Ghi chú (có thể bỏ trống):",
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8)
            });

            var noteBox = new TextBox
            {
                Height = 80,
                AcceptsReturn = true,
                TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
                MaxLength = 300,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto
            };

            panel.Children.Add(noteBox);

            var buttons = new StackPanel
            {
                Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 15, 0, 0)
            };

            var cancelButton = new Button
            {
                Content = "Hủy",
                Width = 90,
                Padding = new Thickness(10, 6, 10, 6),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 0)
            };

            var addButton = new Button
            {
                Content = "Thêm món",
                Width = 100,
                Padding = new Thickness(10, 6, 10, 6),
                Background = Brushes.SaddleBrown,
                Foreground = Brushes.White
            };

            cancelButton.Click += (s, e) =>
                dialog.DialogResult = false;

            addButton.Click += (s, e) =>
                dialog.DialogResult = true;

            buttons.Children.Add(cancelButton);
            buttons.Children.Add(addButton);

            panel.Children.Add(buttons);
            dialog.Content = panel;

            bool? result = dialog.ShowDialog();

            return result == true
                ? noteBox.Text.Trim()
                : null;
        }

        // Hộp thoại xác nhận thanh toán QR.
        private bool ShowQrPaymentDialog(decimal totalAmount)
        {
            var qrWindow = new Window
            {
                Title = "Thanh toán qua Mã QR VietQR",
                Width = 360,
                Height = 440,
                WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
                Owner = Window.GetWindow(this),
                ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize,
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(
                    (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#F7F5F0"))
            };

            var stackPanel = new StackPanel
            {
                Margin = new Thickness(15)
            };

            stackPanel.Children.Add(new TextBlock
            {
                Text = $"Tổng tiền: {totalAmount:N0} đ",
                FontSize = 18,
                FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold,
                Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(
                    (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#C62828")),
                HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center,
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10)
            });

            string localImagePath = Path.Combine(
                AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory,
                "img",
                "QR.jpg");

            var qrImage = new Image
            {
                Width = 240,
                Height = 240,
                Source = new BitmapImage(
                    new Uri(localImagePath, UriKind.Absolute)),
                Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 15)
            };

            stackPanel.Children.Add(qrImage);

            var confirmButton = new Button
            {
                Content = "Đã nhận tiền / In hóa đơn",
                MinHeight = 40,
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(
                    (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#4E342E")),
                Foreground = Brushes.White,
                FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold,
                Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Hand,
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(0)
            };

            confirmButton.Click += (s, e) =>
            {
                qrWindow.DialogResult = true;
            };

            stackPanel.Children.Add(confirmButton);
            qrWindow.Content = stackPanel;

            return qrWindow.ShowDialog() == true;
        }

        // Hiển thị thông báo từ ViewModel.
        private void ShowMessage(
            string message,
            string title,
            bool isError)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                message,
                title,
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                isError
                    ? MessageBoxImage.Error
                    : MessageBoxImage.Information);
        }

        // Chuyển sang màn hình KDS.
        private void NavigateToKds()
        {
            if (Window.GetWindow(this) is MainShellWindow mainWindow)
            {
                mainWindow.OpenKds();
            }
        }
    }

using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class OrderView : Page
    {
        private OrderViewModel? _initializedVm;

        public OrderView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            DataContextChanged += OrderView_DataContextChanged;
        }

        private async void OrderView_DataContextChanged(
            object sender,
            DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.NewValue is not OrderViewModel vm)
                return;

            if (ReferenceEquals(_initializedVm, vm))
                return;

            _initializedVm = vm;

            await vm.InitializeAsync();
        }
    }
}

using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using CoffeeRuu.Repositories;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class MenuView : UserControl
    {
        private readonly MenuService _menuService;

        public MenuView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            // Khởi tạo Repository.
            var repo = new MenuRepository(App.CreateDbContext());

            // Khởi tạo Service.
            _menuService = new MenuService(repo);

            // Chờ View được gắn vào Window rồi mới lấy Owner.
            Loaded += MenuView_Loaded;
        }

        private async void MenuView_Loaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            Loaded -= MenuView_Loaded;

            // Khởi tạo DialogService khi đã có Window.
            var dialogService = new MenuDialogService(
                Window.GetWindow(this),
                _menuService);

            // Khởi tạo ViewModel.
            var viewModel = new MenuViewModel(
                _menuService,
                dialogService);

            DataContext = viewModel;

            // Tải dữ liệu.
            await viewModel.LoadProductsAsync();
        }

        private void ComboBox_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
        {

        }
    }

using System.Windows;
using CoffeeRuu.Repositories;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class MainShellWindow : Window
    {
        private readonly NavigationService _navigationService;
        private readonly OrderService _orderService;

        public MainShellWindow()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            _navigationService = new NavigationService(MainContent);

            _orderService = new OrderService(
                new OrderRepository(App.CreateDbContext));

            RegisterViews();

            DataContext = new MainWindowViewModel(
                _navigationService,
                new WindowService(this));

            _navigationService.Navigate("Home");
        }

        private void RegisterViews()
        {
            _navigationService.Register(
                "Home",
                () => new HomeView());

            _navigationService.Register(
                "Menu",
                () => new PosMenuView());

            _navigationService.Register(
                "Product",
                () => new ProductManagementView());

            _navigationService.Register(
                "Orders",
                () => new OrderView
                {
                    DataContext = new OrderViewModel(_orderService)
                });

            _navigationService.Register(
                "KDS",
                () => new KdsView
                {
                    DataContext = new KdsViewModel(_orderService)
                });

            _navigationService.Register(
                "Inventory",
                () => new InventoryView());

            _navigationService.Register(
    "RecipeManagement",
    () => new RecipeManagementView
    {
        DataContext = new RecipeManagementViewModel(
            new RecipeService(App.CreateDbContext),
            new MessageDialogService())
    });

            _navigationService.Register(
    "Report",
    () => new ReportView
    {
        DataContext = new ReportViewModel(
            new ReportService(App.CreateDbContext),
            new ExcelExportService(),
            new ReportDialogService())
    });

            _navigationService.Register(
                "Table",
                () => new TableView());

            _navigationService.Register(
                "Employee",
                () => new EmployeeManagementView());
        }

        public void OpenKds()
        {
            _navigationService.Navigate("KDS");
        }
    }
}

    public partial class LowStockAlertView : Page

        public LowStockAlertView()
        {

            var service =
                new LowStockAlertService(repository);

        }
    }

using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class KdsView : Page
    {
        private KdsViewModel? _initializedVm;

        public KdsView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            DataContextChanged += KdsView_DataContextChanged;
            IsVisibleChanged += KdsView_IsVisibleChanged;
            Unloaded += KdsView_Unloaded;
        }

        private async void KdsView_DataContextChanged(
            object sender,
            DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.OldValue is KdsViewModel oldVm)
            {
                oldVm.StopAutoRefresh();
            }

            if (e.NewValue is not KdsViewModel vm)
                return;

            if (ReferenceEquals(_initializedVm, vm))
                return;

            _initializedVm = vm;

            await vm.InitializeAsync();

            if (IsVisible && ReferenceEquals(DataContext, vm))
            {
                vm.StartAutoRefresh();
            }
        }

        private void KdsView_IsVisibleChanged(
            object sender,
            DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (DataContext is not KdsViewModel vm)
                return;

            if (IsVisible)
            {
                vm.StartAutoRefresh();
            }
            else
            {
                vm.StopAutoRefresh();
            }
        }

        private void KdsView_Unloaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (DataContext is KdsViewModel vm)
            {
                vm.StopAutoRefresh();
            }
        }
    }
}

using CoffeeRuu.Repositories;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System;


namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class InventoryView : Page
    {
        public InventoryView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            // F6.1 - Quản lý nguyên liệu
            IngredientFrame.Navigate(
                new IngredientView());

            // F6.1 - Quản lý nhà cung cấp
            SupplierFrame.Navigate(
                new SupplierView());

            // F6.2 - Nhập hàng
            InitializeStockReceipt();
            
            //F6.3
            LowStockAlertFrame.Navigate(new LowStockAlertView());
        }


        private void InitializeStockReceipt()
        {
            // Lấy nhân viên từ phiên đăng nhập
            var currentUser = UserSession.Current;

            if (currentUser == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Vui lòng đăng nhập để sử dụng chức năng nhập kho.");
            }

            var repository = new StockReceiptRepository(
                App.CreateDbContext());

            var service = new StockReceiptService(repository);

            var viewModel = new StockReceiptViewModel(
                service,
                currentUser.EmployeeId);

            StockReceiptFrame.Navigate(
                new StockReceiptView(viewModel));
        }

    }
}

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class IngredientView : Page
    {
        public IngredientView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            var repository = new IngredientRepository(
                App.CreateDbContext
            );

            var service = new InventoryService(repository);

            DataContext = new IngredientViewModel(service);
        }
    }

using System;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Views
{
    public partial class HomeView : UserControl
    {
        public HomeView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            var session = UserSession.Current;

            if (session == null)
            {
                GreetingText.Text = "Xin chào!";
                RoleTitleText.Text = "Chưa có thông tin vai trò";
                RoleDescriptionText.Text =
                    "Vui lòng đăng nhập để sử dụng các chức năng của CoffeeRuu.";

                return;
            }

            DataContext = session;

            string fullName = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(session.FullName)
                ? session.Username
                : session.FullName;

            GreetingText.Text = $"Xin chào, {fullName}!";

            string role = session.RoleName.Trim();

            switch (role.ToLowerInvariant())
            {
                case "system administrator":
                case "admin":
                    RoleTitleText.Text = "Quản trị hệ thống";

                    RoleDescriptionText.Text =
                        "Bạn có thể sử dụng chức năng Employee để quản lý tài khoản nhân viên, " +
                        "cập nhật thông tin và phân quyền truy cập. Chức năng System Logs " +
                        "dùng để theo dõi các hoạt động quan trọng của hệ thống.";
                    break;

                case "cashier":
                    RoleTitleText.Text = "Nhân viên thu ngân";

                    RoleDescriptionText.Text =
                        "Bạn có thể sử dụng Menu để xem thông tin sản phẩm, Orders để xử lý đơn hàng, " +
                        "Table để theo dõi và quản lý bàn, cùng Report để xem các báo cáo được cấp quyền. " +
                        "Hãy kiểm tra thông tin đơn hàng và thanh toán trước khi hoàn tất giao dịch.";
                    break;

                case "barista":
                    RoleTitleText.Text = "Nhân viên pha chế";

                    RoleDescriptionText.Text =
                        "Bạn có thể sử dụng Menu để xem thông tin món uống, kích cỡ và topping; " +
                        "sử dụng KDS (Kitchen Display System) để theo dõi các đơn cần pha chế. " +
                        "Hãy kiểm tra yêu cầu của khách hàng, chuẩn bị món theo thông tin đơn hàng " +
                        "và cập nhật trạng thái pha chế khi chức năng này được cung cấp.";
                    break;

                case "warehouse manager":
                    RoleTitleText.Text = "Quản lý kho";

                    RoleDescriptionText.Text =
                        "Bạn có thể sử dụng Inventory để theo dõi nguyên liệu và tình trạng tồn kho. " +
                        "Các chức năng nhập kho và giao dịch tồn kho hỗ trợ ghi nhận biến động nguyên liệu. " +
                        "Hãy kiểm tra số lượng tồn để kịp thời phát hiện nguyên liệu cần bổ sung.";
                    break;

                default:
                    RoleTitleText.Text = role;

                    RoleDescriptionText.Text =
                        "Các chức năng hiển thị trên hệ thống phụ thuộc vào quyền truy cập " +
                        "được cấp cho tài khoản của bạn.";
                    break;
            }
        }
    }
}

                                <DataGridTemplateColumn.CellTemplate>
                                    <DataTemplate>
                                        <Border CornerRadius="5" Padding="7,4"
                                                HorizontalAlignment="Center">
                                            <Border.Style>
                                                <Style TargetType="{x:Type Border}">
                                                    <Setter Property="Background" Value="#F5E5E2"/>
                                                    <Style.Triggers>
                                                        <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding IsActive}" Value="True">
                                                            <Setter Property="Background" Value="#E4EEE5"/>
                                                        </DataTrigger>
                                                    </Style.Triggers>
                                                </Style>
                                            </Border.Style>

                                            <TextBlock FontSize="10" FontWeight="SemiBold">
                                                <TextBlock.Style>
                                                    <Style TargetType="{x:Type TextBlock}">
                                                        <Setter Property="Text" Value="Ngừng hoạt động"/>
                                                        <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="#A64F46"/>
                                                        <Style.Triggers>
                                                            <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding IsActive}" Value="True">
                                                                <Setter Property="Text" Value="Đang hoạt động"/>
                                                                <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="#426C4E"/>
                                                            </DataTrigger>
                                                        </Style.Triggers>
                                                    </Style>
                                                </TextBlock.Style>
                                            </TextBlock>
                                        </Border>
                                    </DataTemplate>
                                </DataGridTemplateColumn.CellTemplate>
                            </DataGridTemplateColumn>

                            <DataGridTemplateColumn Header="Thao tác" Width="100">
                                <DataGridTemplateColumn.CellTemplate>
                                    <DataTemplate>
                                        <Button Content="Chỉnh sửa"
                                                Command="{Binding DataContext.EditEmployeeCommand, RelativeSource={RelativeSource AncestorType={x:Type DataGrid}}}"
                                                CommandParameter="{Binding}"
                                                Style="{StaticResource CoffeeButton}"
                                                Height="30" Padding="8,0" FontSize="11"
                                                Background="#E9DFD9"
                                                Foreground="{StaticResource CoffeeBrown}"/>
                                    </DataTemplate>
                                </DataGridTemplateColumn.CellTemplate>
                            </DataGridTemplateColumn>
                        </DataGrid.Columns>
                    </DataGrid>

                    <TextBlock Grid.Row="3"
                               Text="{Binding FilteredCount, StringFormat=Đang hiển thị {0} tài khoản}"
                               Foreground="{StaticResource MutedText}"
                               FontSize="12" Margin="0,12,0,0"/>
                </Grid>
            </Border>

            <!-- Form them va sua nhan vien -->
            <Border Grid.Column="2"
                    Background="White"
                    BorderBrush="{StaticResource BorderColor}"
                    BorderThickness="1" CornerRadius="10"
                    Padding="18"
                    Visibility="{Binding FormVisibility}">
                <ScrollViewer VerticalScrollBarVisibility="Auto">
                    <StackPanel>
                        <TextBlock Text="{Binding FormTitle}"
                                   FontSize="20" FontWeight="Bold"
                                   Foreground="{StaticResource CoffeeDark}"
                                   Margin="0,0,0,5"/>
                        <TextBlock Text="Nhập thông tin tài khoản nhân viên"
                                   FontSize="12"
                                   Foreground="{StaticResource MutedText}"
                                   TextWrapping="Wrap" Margin="0,0,0,18"/>

                        <TextBlock Text="Tên đăng nhập" FontSize="12"
                                   FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource TextColor}"/>
                        <TextBox Text="{Binding Username, UpdateSourceTrigger=PropertyChanged}"
                                 Style="{StaticResource CoffeeTextBox}" Margin="0,6,0,13"/>

                        <TextBlock Text="Mật khẩu" FontSize="12"
                                   FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource TextColor}"/>
                        <Grid Margin="0,6,0,5">
                            <PasswordBox x:Name="PasswordInput"
                                         PasswordChanged="PasswordInput_PasswordChanged"
                                         Height="36" Padding="10,6,36,6"
                                         VerticalContentAlignment="Center"
                                         BorderBrush="{StaticResource BorderColor}"
                                         BorderThickness="1" Background="White"/>
                            <TextBox x:Name="PasswordTextInput"
                                     TextChanged="PasswordTextInput_TextChanged"
                                     Style="{StaticResource CoffeeTextBox}"
                                     Padding="10,6,36,6"
                                     Visibility="Collapsed"/>
                            <Button x:Name="TogglePasswordButton"
                                    Content="👁" Width="30" Height="30"
                                    HorizontalAlignment="Right" VerticalAlignment="Center"
                                    Margin="0,0,3,0" Background="Transparent"
                                    Foreground="{StaticResource CoffeeBrown}"
                                    BorderThickness="0" Cursor="Hand"
                                    Click="TogglePasswordButton_Click"
                                    ToolTip="Hiện hoặc ẩn mật khẩu"/>
                        </Grid>
                        <TextBlock Text="Nhập mật khẩu mới khi đổi mật khẩu."
                                   Foreground="{StaticResource MutedText}" FontSize="10"
                                   TextWrapping="Wrap" Margin="0,0,0,13"/>

                        <TextBlock Text="Họ và tên" FontSize="12"
                                   FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource TextColor}"/>
                        <TextBox Text="{Binding FullName, UpdateSourceTrigger=PropertyChanged}"
                                 Style="{StaticResource CoffeeTextBox}" Margin="0,6,0,13"/>

                        <TextBlock Text="Số điện thoại" FontSize="12"
                                   FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource TextColor}"/>
                        <TextBox Text="{Binding Phone, UpdateSourceTrigger=PropertyChanged}"
                                 Style="{StaticResource CoffeeTextBox}" Margin="0,6,0,13"/>

                        <TextBlock Text="Email" FontSize="12"
                                   FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource TextColor}"/>
                        <TextBox Text="{Binding Email, UpdateSourceTrigger=PropertyChanged}"
                                 Style="{StaticResource CoffeeTextBox}" Margin="0,6,0,13"/>

                        <TextBlock Text="Vai trò" FontSize="12"
                                   FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource TextColor}"/>
                        <Grid Margin="0,6,0,13">
                            <ComboBox ItemsSource="{Binding RoleOptions}"
                                      SelectedItem="{Binding SelectedRole, Mode=TwoWay}"
                                      DisplayMemberPath="RoleName">
                                <ComboBox.Style>
                                    <Style TargetType="{x:Type ComboBox}"
                                           BasedOn="{StaticResource CoffeeComboBox}">
                                        <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Visible"/>
                                        <Style.Triggers>
                                            <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding IsSelectedAdmin}" Value="True">
                                                <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Collapsed"/>
                                            </DataTrigger>
                                        </Style.Triggers>
                                    </Style>
                                </ComboBox.Style>
                            </ComboBox>

                            <TextBox Text="{Binding SelectedRole.RoleName}" IsReadOnly="True">
                                <TextBox.Style>
                                    <Style TargetType="{x:Type TextBox}"
                                           BasedOn="{StaticResource CoffeeTextBox}">
                                        <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Collapsed"/>
                                        <Style.Triggers>
                                            <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding IsSelectedAdmin}" Value="True">
                                                <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Visible"/>
                                            </DataTrigger>
                                        </Style.Triggers>
                                    </Style>
                                </TextBox.Style>
                            </TextBox>
                        </Grid>

                        <CheckBox Content="Tài khoản đang hoạt động"
                                  IsChecked="{Binding IsActive}"
                                  Foreground="{StaticResource TextColor}"
                                  FontSize="12" Margin="0,0,0,18">
                            <CheckBox.Style>
                                <Style TargetType="{x:Type CheckBox}">
                                    <Setter Property="IsEnabled" Value="True"/>
                                    <Style.Triggers>
                                        <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding IsSelectedAdmin}" Value="True">
                                            <Setter Property="IsEnabled" Value="False"/>
                                        </DataTrigger>
                                    </Style.Triggers>
                                </Style>
                            </CheckBox.Style>
                        </CheckBox>

                        <!-- Nut them moi -->
                        <Button Content="➕  Lưu nhân viên mới"
                                Command="{Binding AddCommand}"
                                Height="40" Margin="0,0,0,8">
                            <Button.Style>
                                <Style TargetType="{x:Type Button}"
                                       BasedOn="{StaticResource CoffeeButton}">
                                    <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Collapsed"/>
                                    <Style.Triggers>
                                        <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding IsAddMode}" Value="True">
                                            <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Visible"/>
                                        </DataTrigger>
                                    </Style.Triggers>
                                </Style>
                            </Button.Style>
                        </Button>

                        <!-- Nut cap nhat -->
                        <Button Content="💾  Lưu thay đổi"
                                Command="{Binding UpdateCommand}"
                                IsEnabled="{Binding CanUpdate}"
                                Height="40" Margin="0,0,0,8">
                            <Button.Style>
                                <Style TargetType="{x:Type Button}"
                                       BasedOn="{StaticResource CoffeeButton}">
                                    <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Collapsed"/>
                                    <Setter Property="Background" Value="#76584B"/>
                                    <Style.Triggers>
                                        <DataTrigger Binding="{Binding IsAddMode}" Value="False">
                                            <Setter Property="Visibility" Value="Visible"/>
                                        </DataTrigger>
                                    </Style.Triggers>
                                </Style>
                            </Button.Style>
                        </Button>


                        <Button Content="Hủy"
                                Command="{Binding CancelCommand}"
                                Height="38"
                                Background="#E9DFD9"
                                Foreground="{StaticResource CoffeeBrown}"
                                BorderThickness="0"
                                Cursor="Hand"/>
                    </StackPanel>
                </ScrollViewer>
            </Border>
        </Grid>

        <!-- Thong bao -->
        <TextBlock Grid.Row="3"
                   Text="{Binding StatusMessage}"
                   Foreground="{StaticResource CoffeeBrown}"
                   FontSize="12" Margin="2,12,0,0"
                   TextWrapping="Wrap"/>
    </Grid>
</UserControl>
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class TableViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly TableService _tableService;

        public ObservableCollection<CafeTable> Tables { get; } = new();

        // Chỉ hiển thị 3 trạng thái tiếng Việt theo yêu cầu
        public ObservableCollection<string> StatusList { get; } = new()
        {
            "Đang có sẵn", "Đã đặt", "Đang bảo trì"
        };

        private CafeTable? _selectedTable;
        public CafeTable? SelectedTable
        {
            get => _selectedTable;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedTable == value) return;

                _selectedTable = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();

                if (value != null)
                {
                    TableNameInput = value.TableName;
                    CapacityInput = value.Capacity.ToString();
                    // Chuyển DB status thành Tiếng Việt để hiển thị
                    StatusInput = MapStatusToVietnamese(value.TableStatus);
                }
                else
                {
                    // Tinh gọn, chỉ gọi ClearInputs 1 lần
                    ClearInputs(false);
                }

                UpdateCommands();
            }
        }

        private string _tableNameInput = "";
        public string TableNameInput
        {
            get => _tableNameInput;
            set { _tableNameInput = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
        }

        private string _capacityInput = "4";
        public string CapacityInput
        {
            get => _capacityInput;
            set { _capacityInput = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
        }

        private string _statusInput = "Đang có sẵn";
        public string StatusInput
        {
            get => _statusInput;
            set { _statusInput = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
        }

        private string _statusMessage = "";
        public string StatusMessage
        {
            get => _statusMessage;
            set { _statusMessage = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
        }

        public RelayCommand AddCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand LoadCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand UpdateCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand DeleteCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand ClearCommand { get; }

        public TableViewModel(TableService tableService)
        {
            _tableService = tableService;

            AddCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await AddTableAsync(), () => SelectedTable == null);
            UpdateCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await UpdateTableAsync(), () => SelectedTable != null);
            DeleteCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await DeleteTableAsync(), () => SelectedTable != null);

            // Lệnh làm mới chỉ reset UI, không tải lại danh sách bàn (giảm tải DB)
            ClearCommand = new RelayCommand(() => ClearInputs(true));

            LoadCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await LoadTablesAsync());

            _ = LoadTablesAsync();
        }

        // --- Helper Methods ---

        private string GetNextTableName()
        {
            if (Tables == null || Tables.Count == 0) return "T01";

            int maxNumber = 0;
            foreach (var table in Tables)
            {
                string numbers = new string(table.TableName.Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
                if (int.TryParse(numbers, out int num) && num > maxNumber)
                {
                    maxNumber = num;
                }
            }
            return $"T{(maxNumber + 1):D2}";
        }

        // Map Tiếng Anh từ DB sang Tiếng Việt
        private string MapStatusToVietnamese(string dbStatus)
        {
            return dbStatus switch
            {
                "Available" => "Đang có sẵn",
                "Reserved" => "Đã đặt",
                "OutOfService" => "Đang bảo trì",
                // Mặc định, mapping Occupied về Đã đặt cho khớp với yêu cầu 3 trạng thái của bạn, hoặc bạn có thể tự chỉnh
                "Occupied" => "Đã đặt",
                _ => "Đang có sẵn"
            };
        }

        // Map Tiếng Việt sang Tiếng Anh lưu vào DB
        private string MapStatusToDatabase(string viStatus)
        {
            return viStatus switch
            {
                "Đang có sẵn" => "Available",
                "Đã đặt" => "Reserved",
                "Đang bảo trì" => "OutOfService",
                _ => "Available"
            };
        }

        // --- Commands ---

        private async Task AddTableAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!int.TryParse(CapacityInput, out int capacity))
                    throw new ArgumentException("Sức chứa phải là số nguyên.");

                string newTableName = GetNextTableName();
                // Map trạng thái tiếng Việt sang tiếng Anh trước khi lưu DB
                string dbStatus = MapStatusToDatabase(StatusInput);

                await _tableService.AddTableAsync(newTableName, capacity, dbStatus);

                await LoadTablesAsync();
                StatusMessage = $"Thêm {newTableName} thành công!";
            }
            catch (Exception ex) { StatusMessage = ex.Message; }
        }

        private async Task LoadTablesAsync()
        {
            var items = await _tableService.GetTablesAsync();
            Tables.Clear();
            foreach (var item in items)
            {
                // Cập nhật lại trạng thái bằng Tiếng Việt trực tiếp trên Model để DataGrid hiển thị đẹp
                item.TableStatus = MapStatusToVietnamese(item.TableStatus);
                Tables.Add(item);
            }

            if (SelectedTable == null)
            {
                ClearInputs(false);
            }
        }

        private async Task UpdateTableAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                if (SelectedTable == null) return;
                if (!int.TryParse(CapacityInput, out int capacity))
                    throw new ArgumentException("Sức chứa phải là số nguyên.");

                // Map trạng thái tiếng Việt sang tiếng Anh
                string dbStatus = MapStatusToDatabase(StatusInput);

                await _tableService.UpdateTableAsync(SelectedTable.TableId, TableNameInput, capacity);
                await _tableService.ChangeTableStatusAsync(SelectedTable.TableId, dbStatus);

                await LoadTablesAsync();
                StatusMessage = "Cập nhật bàn thành công!";
            }
            catch (Exception ex) { StatusMessage = ex.Message; }
        }

        private async Task DeleteTableAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                if (SelectedTable == null) return;

                await _tableService.DeleteTableAsync(SelectedTable.TableId);
                await LoadTablesAsync();
                StatusMessage = "Xóa bàn thành công!";
            }
            catch (Exception ex) { StatusMessage = ex.Message; }
        }

        private void ClearInputs(bool isFromCommand)
        {
            // Tránh vòng lặp khi Set SelectedTable = null sẽ gọi lại ClearInputs
            if (isFromCommand && _selectedTable != null)
            {
                _selectedTable = null;
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedTable));
            }

            TableNameInput = GetNextTableName();
            CapacityInput = "4";
            StatusInput = "Đang có sẵn";

            // Xóa message lỗi nếu người dùng nhấn nút làm mới
            if (isFromCommand) StatusMessage = "";

            UpdateCommands();
        }

        private void UpdateCommands()
        {
            AddCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            UpdateCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            DeleteCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            ClearCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
        }

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;
        protected void OnPropertyChanged([CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }
}
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Data;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class SupplierViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly SupplierService _supplierService;

        // Danh sách nhà cung cấp
        public ObservableCollection<Supplier> Suppliers
        { get; } = new();

        public ICollectionView SuppliersView { get; }

        // Nhà cung cấp được chọn
        private Supplier? _selectedSupplier;

        public Supplier? SelectedSupplier
        {
            get => _selectedSupplier;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedSupplier == value)
                    return;

                _selectedSupplier = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();

                if (value != null)
                {
                    SupplierNameInput = value.SupplierName;
                    PhoneInput = value.Phone ?? "";
                    EmailInput = value.Email ?? "";
                    AddressInput = value.Address ?? "";
                }
                else
                {
                    ClearInputs();
                }

                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ToggleButtonText));
                UpdateCommands();
            }
        }

        // Thông tin nhập từ form
        private string _supplierNameInput = "";

        public string SupplierNameInput
        {
            get => _supplierNameInput;
            set
            {
                _supplierNameInput = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _phoneInput = "";

        public string PhoneInput
        {
            get => _phoneInput;
            set
            {
                _phoneInput = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _emailInput = "";

        public string EmailInput
        {
            get => _emailInput;
            set
            {
                _emailInput = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _addressInput = "";

        public string AddressInput
        {
            get => _addressInput;
            set
            {
                _addressInput = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        // Tìm kiếm nhà cung cấp
        private string _searchText = "";

        public string SearchText
        {
            get => _searchText;
            set
            {
                _searchText = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                SuppliersView.Refresh();
            }
        }

        // Trạng thái xử lý
        private bool _isBusy;

        public bool IsBusy
        {
            get => _isBusy;
            private set
            {
                _isBusy = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                UpdateCommands();
            }
        }

        private string _statusMessage = "";

        public string StatusMessage
        {
            get => _statusMessage;
            set
            {
                _statusMessage = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        // Nội dung nút thay đổi trạng thái
        public string ToggleButtonText =>
            SelectedSupplier?.IsActive == true
                ? "Ngừng hợp tác"
                : "Kích hoạt lại";

        // Commands
        public RelayCommand LoadCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand AddCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand UpdateCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand ToggleActiveCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand ClearCommand { get; }

        // Constructor
        public SupplierViewModel(
            SupplierService supplierService)
        {
            _supplierService = supplierService;

            SuppliersView =
                CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(Suppliers);

            SuppliersView.Filter = FilterSupplier;

            LoadCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    LoadSuppliersAsync),
                () => !IsBusy);

            AddCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    AddSupplierAsync),
                () => !IsBusy && SelectedSupplier == null);

            UpdateCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    UpdateSupplierAsync),
                () => !IsBusy && SelectedSupplier != null);

            ToggleActiveCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    ToggleActiveAsync),
                () => !IsBusy && SelectedSupplier != null);

            ClearCommand = new RelayCommand(
                ClearForm,
                () => !IsBusy);

            // Tải dữ liệu khi tạo ViewModel
            _ = ExecuteSafeAsync(LoadSuppliersAsync);
        }

        // 1. Lấy danh sách nhà cung cấp
        private async Task LoadSuppliersAsync()
        {
            int? selectedId = SelectedSupplier?.SupplierId;

            var items = await _supplierService
                .GetSuppliersAsync();

            Suppliers.Clear();

            foreach (var item in items)
            {
                Suppliers.Add(item);
            }

            SelectedSupplier = selectedId.HasValue
                ? Suppliers.FirstOrDefault(
                    s => s.SupplierId == selectedId.Value)
                : null;

            SuppliersView.Refresh();
        }

        // 2. Thêm nhà cung cấp
        private async Task AddSupplierAsync()
        {
            await _supplierService.AddSupplierAsync(
                SupplierNameInput,
                PhoneInput,
                EmailInput,
                AddressInput);

            await LoadSuppliersAsync();

            ClearForm();

            StatusMessage = "Thêm nhà cung cấp thành công.";
        }

        // 3. Cập nhật nhà cung cấp
        private async Task UpdateSupplierAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedSupplier == null)
                return;

            int id = SelectedSupplier.SupplierId;

            await _supplierService.UpdateSupplierAsync(
                id,
                SupplierNameInput,
                PhoneInput,
                EmailInput,
                AddressInput);

            await LoadSuppliersAsync();

            StatusMessage = "Cập nhật nhà cung cấp thành công.";
        }

        // 4. Kích hoạt / ngừng hợp tác
        private async Task ToggleActiveAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedSupplier == null)
                return;

            bool newStatus = !SelectedSupplier.IsActive;

            await _supplierService.SetSupplierActiveAsync(
                SelectedSupplier.SupplierId,
                newStatus);

            await LoadSuppliersAsync();

            StatusMessage = newStatus
                ? "Đã kích hoạt nhà cung cấp."
                : "Đã ngừng hợp tác với nhà cung cấp.";
        }

        // 5. Lọc danh sách nhà cung cấp
        private bool FilterSupplier(object obj)
        {
            if (obj is not Supplier supplier)
                return false;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SearchText))
                return true;

            string keyword = SearchText.Trim();

            return supplier.SupplierName.Contains(
                       keyword,
                       StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                   || (supplier.Phone?.Contains(
                       keyword,
                       StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false)
                   || (supplier.Email?.Contains(
                       keyword,
                       StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false);
        }

        // 6. Xóa thông tin nhập
        private void ClearInputs()
        {
            SupplierNameInput = "";
            PhoneInput = "";
            EmailInput = "";
            AddressInput = "";
        }

        private void ClearForm()
        {
            SelectedSupplier = null;
            ClearInputs();
        }

        // 7. Xử lý thao tác bất đồng bộ
        private async Task ExecuteSafeAsync(Func<Task> action)
        {
            if (IsBusy)
                return;

            try
            {
                IsBusy = true;
                StatusMessage = "";

                await action();
            }
            catch (ArgumentException ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = ex.Message;
            }
            catch (InvalidOperationException ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = ex.Message;
            }
            catch (KeyNotFoundException ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = ex.Message;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage =
                    "Có lỗi xảy ra: " + ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                IsBusy = false;
            }
        }

        // 8. Cập nhật trạng thái các Command
        private void UpdateCommands()
        {
            LoadCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            AddCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            UpdateCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            ToggleActiveCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            ClearCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
        }

        // 9. Thông báo thay đổi thuộc tính
        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler?
            PropertyChanged;

        private void OnPropertyChanged(
            [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }
}

using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels;

public class StockReceiptLineViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
{
    public ObservableCollection<Ingredient> AvailableIngredients
    { get; } = new();

    private Ingredient? _selectedIngredient;
    private string _quantityText = "";
    private string _unitCostText = "";

    public Ingredient? SelectedIngredient
    {
        get => _selectedIngredient;
        set
        {
            _selectedIngredient = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
        }
    }

    public string QuantityText
    {
        get => _quantityText;
        set
        {
            _quantityText = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LineTotal));
        }
    }

    public string UnitCostText
    {
        get => _unitCostText;
        set
        {
            _unitCostText = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LineTotal));
        }
    }

    // Chuyển dữ liệu người dùng nhập thành decimal
    public bool TryGetValues(
        out decimal quantity,
        out decimal unitCost)
    {
        bool validQuantity = decimal.TryParse(
            QuantityText,
            NumberStyles.Number,
            CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
            out quantity);

        bool validCost = decimal.TryParse(
            UnitCostText,
            NumberStyles.Number,
            CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
            out unitCost);

        return validQuantity
            && validCost
            && quantity > 0
            && unitCost >= 0
            && decimal.Round(quantity, 3) == quantity
            && decimal.Round(unitCost, 2) == unitCost;
    }

    // Thành tiền của dòng nguyên liệu
    public decimal LineTotal
    {
        get
        {
            if (!TryGetValues(out var quantity, out var unitCost))
                return 0;

            try
            {
                return quantity * unitCost;
            }
            catch (OverflowException)
            {
                return 0;
            }
        }
    }

    public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

    private void OnPropertyChanged(
        [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
    {
        PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
            this,
            new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
    }
}

public class StockReceiptViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
{
    private readonly StockReceiptService _service;
    private readonly int _currentEmployeeId;

    public ObservableCollection<Supplier> Suppliers { get; } = new();
    public ObservableCollection<Ingredient> Ingredients { get; } = new();

    public ObservableCollection<StockReceiptLineViewModel> Lines
    { get; } = new();

    public ObservableCollection<StockReceipt> RecentReceipts
    { get; } = new();

    private Supplier? _selectedSupplier;
    public Supplier? SelectedSupplier
    {
        get => _selectedSupplier;
        set
        {
            _selectedSupplier = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
        }
    }

    private StockReceiptLineViewModel? _selectedLine;
    public StockReceiptLineViewModel? SelectedLine
    {
        get => _selectedLine;
        set
        {
            _selectedLine = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
            RemoveLineCommand.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
        }
    }

    private string _note = "";
    public string Note
    {
        get => _note;
        set
        {
            _note = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
        }
    }

    private string _statusMessage = "";
    public string StatusMessage
    {
        get => _statusMessage;
        set
        {
            _statusMessage = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
        }
    }

    private bool _isBusy;
    public bool IsBusy
    {
        get => _isBusy;
        private set
        {
            _isBusy = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();

            AddLineCommand.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            RemoveLineCommand.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            SaveCommand.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            RefreshCommand.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
        }
    }

    public decimal TotalCost
    {
        get
        {
            try
            {
                return decimal.Round(
                    Lines.Sum(x => x.LineTotal),
                    2,
                    MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero);
            }
            catch (OverflowException)
            {
                return 0;
            }
        }
    }

    public RelayCommand AddLineCommand { get; }
    public RelayCommand RemoveLineCommand { get; }
    public RelayCommand SaveCommand { get; }
    public RelayCommand RefreshCommand { get; }

    public StockReceiptViewModel(
        StockReceiptService service,
        int currentEmployeeId)
    {
        _service = service;
        _currentEmployeeId = currentEmployeeId;

        AddLineCommand = new RelayCommand(
            AddLine,
            () => !IsBusy);

        RemoveLineCommand = new RelayCommand(
            RemoveSelectedLine,
            () => !IsBusy && SelectedLine != null);

        SaveCommand = new RelayCommand(
            async () => await SaveAsync(),
            () => !IsBusy);

        RefreshCommand = new RelayCommand(
            async () => await LoadAsync(),
            () => !IsBusy);

        AddLine();
    }

    // Thêm dòng nguyên liệu

    private void AddLine()
    {
        if (IsBusy)
            return;

        var line = new StockReceiptLineViewModel();

        // Cung cấp danh sách nguyên liệu cho dòng mới
        foreach (var ingredient in Ingredients)
        {
            line.AvailableIngredients.Add(ingredient);
        }

        line.PropertyChanged += OnLinePropertyChanged;

        Lines.Add(line);

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalCost));
    }


    // Xóa dòng đang chọn
    private void RemoveSelectedLine()
    {
        if (IsBusy || SelectedLine == null)
            return;

        SelectedLine.PropertyChanged -= OnLinePropertyChanged;

        Lines.Remove(SelectedLine);
        SelectedLine = null;

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalCost));
    }

    // Cập nhật tổng tiền khi số lượng hoặc đơn giá thay đổi
    private void OnLinePropertyChanged(
        object? sender,
        PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.PropertyName == nameof(StockReceiptLineViewModel.LineTotal))
        {
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalCost));
        }
    }

    // Xóa dữ liệu phiếu nhập sau khi lưu thành công
    private void ResetForm()
    {
        SelectedSupplier = null;
        SelectedLine = null;
        Note = "";

        foreach (var line in Lines)
        {
            line.PropertyChanged -= OnLinePropertyChanged;
        }

        Lines.Clear();
        AddLine();

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalCost));
    }

    // Lấy dữ liệu từ database
    private async Task ReloadDataAsync()
    {
        var selectedSupplierId = SelectedSupplier?.SupplierId;

        var lineIngredientIds = Lines
            .Select(x => x.SelectedIngredient?.IngredientId)
            .ToList();

        // Thực hiện lần lượt vì Repository dùng cùng DbContext
        var suppliers = await _service.GetActiveSuppliersAsync();
        var ingredients = await _service.GetActiveIngredientsAsync();
        var receipts = await _service.GetRecentReceiptsAsync();

        Suppliers.Clear();
        foreach (var supplier in suppliers)
            Suppliers.Add(supplier);

        Ingredients.Clear();
        foreach (var ingredient in ingredients)
            Ingredients.Add(ingredient);

        RecentReceipts.Clear();
        foreach (var receipt in receipts)
            RecentReceipts.Add(receipt);

        SelectedSupplier = Suppliers.FirstOrDefault(
            s => s.SupplierId == selectedSupplierId);


        for (int i = 0; i < Lines.Count; i++)
        {
            var line = Lines[i];
            var ingredientId = lineIngredientIds[i];

            line.AvailableIngredients.Clear();

            foreach (var ingredient in Ingredients)
            {
                line.AvailableIngredients.Add(ingredient);
            }

            line.SelectedIngredient =
                line.AvailableIngredients.FirstOrDefault(
                    x => x.IngredientId == ingredientId);
        }

    }

    // Được gọi khi mở trang hoặc nhấn Refresh
    public async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        if (IsBusy)
            return;

        IsBusy = true;
        StatusMessage = "Đang tải dữ liệu...";

        try
        {
            await ReloadDataAsync();

            StatusMessage = "Đã tải dữ liệu nhập hàng.";
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            StatusMessage = $"Không thể tải dữ liệu: {ex.Message}";
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    // Xác nhận nhập hàng
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (IsBusy)
            return;

        if (SelectedSupplier == null)
        {
            StatusMessage = "Vui lòng chọn nhà cung cấp.";
            return;
        }

        if (_currentEmployeeId <= 0)
        {
            StatusMessage = "Không xác định được nhân viên đăng nhập.";
            return;
        }

        if (Lines.Count == 0)
        {
            StatusMessage = "Vui lòng thêm ít nhất một nguyên liệu.";
            return;
        }

        var request = new CreateStockReceiptRequest
        {
            SupplierId = SelectedSupplier.SupplierId,
            ReceivedBy = _currentEmployeeId,
            Note = Note,
            Items = new List<StockReceiptItemRequest>()
        };

        // Kiểm tra từng dòng và tạo request
        for (int i = 0; i < Lines.Count; i++)
        {
            var line = Lines[i];

            if (line.SelectedIngredient == null)
            {
                StatusMessage =
                    $"Dòng {i + 1}: Vui lòng chọn nguyên liệu.";
                return;
            }

            if (!line.TryGetValues(
                out var quantity,
                out var unitCost))
            {
                StatusMessage =
                    $"Dòng {i + 1}: Số lượng hoặc đơn giá không hợp lệ.";
                return;
            }

            request.Items.Add(new StockReceiptItemRequest
            {
                IngredientId = line.SelectedIngredient.IngredientId,
                Quantity = quantity,
                UnitCost = unitCost
            });
        }

        IsBusy = true;
        StatusMessage = "Đang lưu phiếu nhập...";

        int receiptId;

        try
        {
            receiptId = await _service.CreateStockReceiptAsync(request);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            StatusMessage = $"Nhập hàng thất bại: {ex.Message}";
            IsBusy = false;
            return;
        }

        // Đến đây phiếu nhập đã được lưu thành công
        ResetForm();

        try
        {
            await ReloadDataAsync();
            StatusMessage = $"Đã nhập hàng thành công! Mã phiếu: #{receiptId}";
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            StatusMessage =
                $"Đã lưu phiếu #{receiptId}, nhưng không thể tải lại dữ liệu: {ex.Message}";
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

    private void OnPropertyChanged(
        [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
    {
        PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
            this,
            new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
    }
}
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class ReportViewModel : BaseViewModel
    {
        private static readonly CultureInfo ViCulture = CultureInfo.GetCultureInfo("vi-VN");

        private readonly ReportService _reportService;
        private readonly ExcelExportService _excelExportService;
        private readonly IReportDialogService _dialogService;

        // Báo cáo của lần lọc gần nhất: dùng cho export để file luôn khớp dữ liệu đang hiển thị.
        private RevenueReport? _currentReport;

        private DateTime? _startDate = DateTime.Today.AddDays(-6);
        private DateTime? _endDate = DateTime.Today;
        private string _revenueText = "0 ₫";
        private string _orderCountText = "0";
        private string _averageOrderText = "0 ₫";
        private string _statusText = string.Empty;
        private bool _isLoading;
        private IReadOnlyList<DailyRevenueRow> _dailyRows = Array.Empty<DailyRevenueRow>();
        private IReadOnlyList<BestSellingProductRow> _productRows = Array.Empty<BestSellingProductRow>();

        public ReportViewModel(
            ReportService reportService,
            ExcelExportService excelExportService,
            IReportDialogService dialogService)
        {
            _reportService = reportService;
            _excelExportService = excelExportService;
            _dialogService = dialogService;

            LoadCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(LoadAsync);
            ExportCommand = new RelayCommand(Export);
        }

        public DateTime? StartDate
        {
            get => _startDate;
            set => SetProperty(ref _startDate, value);
        }

        public DateTime? EndDate
        {
            get => _endDate;
            set => SetProperty(ref _endDate, value);
        }

        public string RevenueText
        {
            get => _revenueText;
            private set => SetProperty(ref _revenueText, value);
        }

        public string OrderCountText
        {
            get => _orderCountText;
            private set => SetProperty(ref _orderCountText, value);
        }

        public string AverageOrderText
        {
            get => _averageOrderText;
            private set => SetProperty(ref _averageOrderText, value);
        }

        public string StatusText
        {
            get => _statusText;
            private set => SetProperty(ref _statusText, value);
        }

        public bool IsLoading
        {
            get => _isLoading;
            private set => SetProperty(ref _isLoading, value);
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<DailyRevenueRow> DailyRows
        {
            get => _dailyRows;
            private set => SetProperty(ref _dailyRows, value);
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<BestSellingProductRow> ProductRows
        {
            get => _productRows;
            private set => SetProperty(ref _productRows, value);
        }

        public ICommand LoadCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ExportCommand { get; }

        public async Task LoadAsync()
        {
            if (IsLoading)
                return;

            if (StartDate is not DateTime startDate || EndDate is not DateTime endDate)
            {
                _dialogService.ShowWarning("Thiếu ngày",
                    "Vui lòng chọn ngày bắt đầu và ngày kết thúc.");
                return;
            }

            if (startDate.Date > endDate.Date)
            {
                _dialogService.ShowWarning("Khoảng thời gian không hợp lệ",
                    "Ngày bắt đầu không được sau ngày kết thúc.");
                return;
            }

            IsLoading = true;
            try
            {
                RevenueReport report = await _reportService.GetReportAsync(startDate, endDate);
