            set => Set(ref _selectedMenu, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // BÀN ĐƯỢC CHỌN
        // ============================

        private TableChoice? _selectedTable;

        public TableChoice? SelectedTable
        {
            get => _selectedTable;
            set => Set(ref _selectedTable, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // MÓN TRONG GIỎ
        // ============================

        private CartLine? _selectedCart;

        public CartLine? SelectedCart
        {
            get => _selectedCart;
            set => Set(ref _selectedCart, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // ĐƠN HÀNG ĐƯỢC CHỌN
        // ============================

        private OrderListRow? _selectedOrder;

        public OrderListRow? SelectedOrder
        {
            get => _selectedOrder;
            set => Set(ref _selectedOrder, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // LOẠI ĐƠN
        // ============================

        private string _orderType = "Takeaway";

        public string OrderType
        {
            get => _orderType;
            set
            {
                if (Set(ref _orderType, value))
                {
                    if (_orderType == "Takeaway")
                        SelectedTable = null;

                    Notify(nameof(IsDineIn));
                }
            }
        }

        public bool IsDineIn =>
            OrderType == "DineIn";

        // ============================
        // DANH SÁCH NHÂN VIÊN
        // ============================

        private EmployeeChoice? _selectedEmployee;

        public EmployeeChoice? SelectedEmployee
        {
            get => _selectedEmployee;
            set => Set(ref _selectedEmployee, value);
        }

        // Giữ tương thích với giao diện tạo đơn cũ.
        // Mã người thao tác được lấy từ UserSession.
        private string _employeeIdText = "";

        public string EmployeeIdText
        {
            get => _employeeIdText;
            set => Set(ref _employeeIdText, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // SỐ LƯỢNG
        // ============================

        private string _quantityText = "1";

        public string QuantityText
        {
            get => _quantityText;
            set => Set(ref _quantityText, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // GHI CHÚ MÓN
        // ============================

        private string _itemNote = "";

        public string ItemNote
        {
            get => _itemNote;
            set => Set(ref _itemNote, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // GHI CHÚ ĐƠN
        // ============================

        private string _orderNote = "";

        public string OrderNote
        {
            get => _orderNote;
            set => Set(ref _orderNote, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // THÔNG BÁO
        // ============================

        private string _message = "";

        public string Message
        {
            get => _message;
            set => Set(ref _message, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // TRẠNG THÁI XỬ LÝ
        // ============================

        private bool _isBusy;

        public bool IsBusy
        {
            get => _isBusy;
            set => Set(ref _isBusy, value);
        }

        // ============================
        // TỔNG TIỀN
        // ============================

        public decimal CartTotal =>
            Cart.Sum(i => i.LineTotal);

        public string CartTotalText =>
            $"Tổng cộng: {CartTotal:N0} đ";

        // ============================
        // BỘ LỌC ĐƠN HÀNG
        // ============================

        public ICollectionView FilteredOrders { get; }

        public string[] StatusOptions { get; } =
        {
            "Đang xử lý",
            "Tất cả",
            "New",
            "Preparing",
            "Ready",
            "Completed",
            "Cancelled"
        };

        // Mặc định ẩn Completed và Cancelled
        private string _statusFilter = "Đang xử lý";

        public string StatusFilter
        {
            get => _statusFilter;
            set
            {
                if (Set(ref _statusFilter, value))
                    FilteredOrders.Refresh();
            }
        }

        // Tìm mã đơn
        private string _searchOrder = "";

        public string SearchOrder
        {
            get => _searchOrder;
            set
            {
                if (Set(ref _searchOrder, value))
                    FilteredOrders.Refresh();
            }
        }

        // Lọc theo ngày
        private DateTime? _filterDate;

        public DateTime? FilterDate
        {
            get => _filterDate;
            set
            {
                if (Set(ref _filterDate, value))
                    FilteredOrders.Refresh();
            }
        }

        // Kiểm tra điều kiện lọc
        private bool FilterOrder(object obj)
        {
            if (obj is not OrderListRow order)
                return false;

            string keyword =
                SearchOrder?.Trim() ?? "";

            bool matchCode =
                keyword.Length == 0 ||
                order.OrderCode.Contains(
                    keyword,
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            bool matchStatus =
                StatusFilter == "Tất cả" ||

                (
                    StatusFilter == "Đang xử lý" &&
                    (
                        order.OrderStatus == "New" ||
                        order.OrderStatus == "Preparing" ||
                        order.OrderStatus == "Ready"
                    )
                ) ||

                order.OrderStatus == StatusFilter;

            bool matchDate =
                !FilterDate.HasValue ||
                order.CreatedAt.Date ==
                    FilterDate.Value.Date;

            return matchCode &&
                   matchStatus &&
                   matchDate;
        }

        // ============================
        // COMMANDS
        // ============================

        public ICommand AddCommand { get; }
        public ICommand RemoveCommand { get; }
        public ICommand CreateCommand { get; }
        public ICommand RefreshCommand { get; }
        public ICommand CancelCommand { get; }
        public ICommand CompleteCommand { get; }

        // ============================
        // CONSTRUCTOR
        // ============================

        public OrderViewModel(OrderService service)
        {
            _service = service;

            // Tạo bộ lọc cho danh sách đơn
            FilteredOrders =
                CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(Orders);

            FilteredOrders.Filter = FilterOrder;

            AddCommand = new RelayCommand(AddItem);

            RemoveCommand = new RelayCommand(RemoveItem);

            CreateCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await RunAsync(CreateOrderAsync));

            RefreshCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await RunAsync(LoadDataAsync));

            CancelCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await RunAsync(CancelOrderAsync));

            CompleteCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await RunAsync(CompleteOrderAsync));
        }

        public Task InitializeAsync()
        {
            return RunAsync(LoadDataAsync);
        }

        // ============================
        // TẢI DỮ LIỆU
        // ============================

        private async Task LoadDataAsync()
        {
            int? previousEmployeeId =
                SelectedEmployee?.EmployeeId;

            int? previousOrderId =
                SelectedOrder?.OrderId;

            var menu = await _service.GetMenuAsync();
            var tables = await _service.GetTablesAsync();
            var orders = await _service.GetOrdersAsync();
            var employees = await _service.GetEmployeesAsync();

            Replace(Menu, menu);
            Replace(Tables, tables);
            Replace(Orders, orders);
            Replace(Employees, employees);

            // Giữ nhân viên đang chọn
            int? employeeToShow =
                previousEmployeeId ??
                UserSession.Current?.EmployeeId;

            SelectedEmployee = Employees.FirstOrDefault(e =>
                e.EmployeeId == employeeToShow);

            // Mã nhân viên đăng nhập
            EmployeeIdText =
                UserSession.Current?.EmployeeId.ToString() ?? "";

            // Giữ đơn đang chọn nếu còn
            SelectedOrder = Orders.FirstOrDefault(o =>
                o.OrderId == previousOrderId);

            if (SelectedMenu == null ||
                !Menu.Contains(SelectedMenu))
            {
                SelectedMenu = Menu.FirstOrDefault();
            }

            FilteredOrders.Refresh();
        }

        // ============================
        // THÊM MÓN VÀO GIỎ
        // ============================

        private void AddItem()
        {
            if (IsBusy)
                return;

            if (SelectedMenu == null)
            {
                Message = "Vui lòng chọn sản phẩm.";
                return;
            }

            if (!int.TryParse(
                QuantityText, out int quantity) ||
                quantity < 1 || quantity > 100)
            {
                Message =
                    "Số lượng phải từ 1 đến 100.";
                return;
            }

            if (ItemNote.Length > 300)
            {
                Message =
                    "Ghi chú món tối đa 300 ký tự.";
                return;
            }

            Cart.Add(new CartLine
            {
                ProductSizeId =
                    SelectedMenu.ProductSizeId,

                ProductName =
                    SelectedMenu.ProductName,

                SizeName =
                    SelectedMenu.SizeName,

                UnitPrice =
                    SelectedMenu.Price,

                Quantity = quantity,
                Note = ItemNote.Trim()
            });

            QuantityText = "1";
            ItemNote = "";

            NotifyCartTotal();

            Message = "Đã thêm món vào đơn.";
        }

        // ============================
        // XÓA MÓN KHỎI GIỎ
        // ============================

        private void RemoveItem()
        {
            if (IsBusy)
                return;

            if (SelectedCart == null)
            {
                Message = "Hãy chọn món cần xóa.";
                return;
            }

            Cart.Remove(SelectedCart);
            SelectedCart = null;

            NotifyCartTotal();

            Message = "Đã xóa món khỏi đơn.";
        }

        // ============================
        // TẠO ĐƠN
        // ============================

        private async Task CreateOrderAsync()
        {
            int employeeId = GetEmployeeId();

            var order = await _service.CreateOrderAsync(
                Cart.ToList(),
                OrderType,
                SelectedTable?.TableId,
                employeeId,
                OrderNote);

            Cart.Clear();

            OrderNote = "";
            ItemNote = "";
            QuantityText = "1";

            NotifyCartTotal();

            await LoadDataAsync();

            Message =
                $"Đã tạo đơn {order.OrderCode}. " +
                $"Tổng tiền: {order.TotalAmount:N0} đ.";
        }

        // ============================
        // HỦY ĐƠN
        // ============================

        private async Task CancelOrderAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedOrder == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Hãy chọn đơn cần hủy.");
            }

            // Chỉ hủy đơn mới
            if (SelectedOrder.OrderStatus != "New")
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Chỉ được hủy đơn có trạng thái New.");
            }

            int orderId = SelectedOrder.OrderId;

            await _service.CancelNewOrderAsync(
                orderId,
                GetEmployeeId());

            // Làm mới để đơn Cancelled tự ẩn
            await LoadDataAsync();

            SelectedOrder = null;

            Message = "Đã hủy đơn hàng.";
        }

        // ============================
        // BÀN GIAO ĐƠN
        // ============================

        private async Task CompleteOrderAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedOrder == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Hãy chọn đơn cần bàn giao.");
            }

            if (SelectedOrder.OrderStatus != "Ready")
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Chỉ bàn giao khi đơn đã Ready.");
            }

            int orderId = SelectedOrder.OrderId;

            await _service.CompleteOrderAsync(
                orderId,
                GetEmployeeId());

            // Làm mới danh sách sau khi bàn giao
            await LoadDataAsync();

            // Bỏ dòng đang chọn
            SelectedOrder = null;

            Message =
                "Đã bàn giao đơn hàng thành công.";
        }

        // ============================
        // MÃ NHÂN VIÊN THỰC HIỆN
        // ============================

        private int GetEmployeeId()
        {
            var session = UserSession.Current;

            if (session == null ||
                session.EmployeeId <= 0)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Vui lòng đăng nhập trước khi thao tác.");
            }

            if (!session.CanAccess("Orders"))
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Tài khoản không có quyền xử lý đơn hàng.");
            }

            return session.EmployeeId;
        }

        // ============================
        // HÀM HỖ TRỢ
        // ============================

        private void NotifyCartTotal()
        {
            Notify(nameof(CartTotal));
            Notify(nameof(CartTotalText));
        }

        private async Task RunAsync(Func<Task> action)
        {
            if (IsBusy)
                return;

            IsBusy = true;
            Message = "";

            try
            {
                await action();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Message = "Lỗi: " + ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                IsBusy = false;
            }
        }

        private static void Replace<T>(
            ObservableCollection<T> target,
            IEnumerable<T> source)
        {
            target.Clear();

            foreach (var item in source)
                target.Add(item);
        }
    }
}
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Models
{
    public class OrderDto
    {
        public int OrderId { get; set; }
        public string OrderCode { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.Now;
        public decimal TotalAmount { get; set; }
        public decimal CustomerPaid { get; set; }
        public decimal ChangeAmount { get; set; }
        public string PaymentMethod { get; set; } = "Tiền mặt";
        public List<OrderItemDto> Items { get; set; } = new List<OrderItemDto>();
    }

    public class OrderItemDto
    {
        public string ProductName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string SizeName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public decimal Price { get; set; }
        public int Quantity { get; set; }
        public string Note { get; set; } = "";
        public decimal TotalPrice => Price * Quantity;
    }
}using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class MenuChoice
    {
        public int ProductSizeId { get; set; }
        public string ProductName { get; set; } = "";
        public string SizeName { get; set; } = "";
        public decimal Price { get; set; }

        public string Display =>
            $"{ProductName} - {SizeName} ({Price:N0} đ)";
    }

    public class TableChoice
    {
        public int TableId { get; set; }
        public string TableName { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class CartLine : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private int _quantity;

        public int ProductSizeId { get; set; }

        public string ProductName { get; set; } = "";

        public string SizeName { get; set; } = "";

        public string DisplayName =>
            string.IsNullOrEmpty(SizeName)
                ? ProductName
                : $"{ProductName} ({SizeName})";

        public decimal UnitPrice { get; set; }

        public int Quantity
        {
            get => _quantity;
            set
            {
                if (_quantity != value)
                {
                    _quantity = value;
                    OnPropertyChanged();
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LineTotal));
                }
            }
        }

        public string Note { get; set; } = "";

        public decimal LineTotal => UnitPrice * Quantity;

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        protected void OnPropertyChanged(
            [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }

    public class OrderListRow
    {
        public int OrderId { get; set; }
        public string OrderCode { get; set; } = "";
        public string TableName { get; set; } = "";
        public string OrderType { get; set; } = "";
        public string OrderStatus { get; set; } = "";
        public decimal TotalAmount { get; set; }
        public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }
    }

    public class KdsItemRow
    {
        public int OrderItemId { get; set; }
        public int OrderId { get; set; }

        public string OrderCode { get; set; } = "";
        public string TableName { get; set; } = "";

        public string ProductName { get; set; } = "";
        public string SizeName { get; set; } = "";

        public int Quantity { get; set; }
        public string Note { get; set; } = "";

        public string PreparationStatus { get; set; } = "";
        public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class EmployeeChoice
    {
        public int EmployeeId { get; set; }
        public string FullName { get; set; } = "";
        public string RoleName { get; set; } = "";

        public string DisplayName =>
            $"{EmployeeId} - {FullName} ({RoleName})";
    }

}
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public abstract class NotifyBase : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        protected bool Set<T>(
            ref T field,
            T value,
            [CallerMemberName] string? name = null)
        {
            if (EqualityComparer<T>.Default.Equals(field, value))
                return false;

            field = value;

            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));

            return true;
        }

        protected void Notify(
            [CallerMemberName] string? name = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
        }
    }
}
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class MenuViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly MenuService _menuService;
        private readonly IMenuDialogService _dialogService;

        // Lưu toàn bộ danh sách món lấy từ database.
        private List<Product> _allProducts = new List<Product>();

        // Danh sách món đang hiển thị.
        public ObservableCollection<Product> Products { get; }
            = new ObservableCollection<Product>();

        // Danh sách danh mục.
        public ObservableCollection<string> Categories { get; }
            = new ObservableCollection<string>();

        // Các lựa chọn sắp xếp.
        public ObservableCollection<string> SortOptions { get; }
            = new ObservableCollection<string>
            {
                "Mặc định",
                "Giá thấp đến cao",
                "Giá cao đến thấp"
            };

        // Các lựa chọn lọc trạng thái.
        public ObservableCollection<string> AvailabilityOptions { get; }
            = new ObservableCollection<string>
            {
                "Tất cả trạng thái",
                "Đang bán",
                "Ngừng bán"
            };

        // Từ khóa tìm kiếm.
        private string _searchKeyword = "";

        public string SearchKeyword
        {
            get => _searchKeyword;
            set
            {
                if (_searchKeyword != value)
                {
                    _searchKeyword = value ?? "";
                    OnPropertyChanged();
                }
            }
        }

        // Danh mục đang chọn.
        private string _selectedCategory = "Tất cả";

        public string SelectedCategory
        {
            get => _selectedCategory;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedCategory != value)
                {
                    _selectedCategory = value ?? "Tất cả";
                    OnPropertyChanged();

                    ApplyFilters();
                }
            }
        }

        // Kiểu sắp xếp đang chọn.
        private string _selectedSort = "Mặc định";

        public string SelectedSort
        {
            get => _selectedSort;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedSort != value)
                {
                    _selectedSort = value ?? "Mặc định";
                    OnPropertyChanged();

                    ApplyFilters();
                }
            }
        }

        // Trạng thái bán đang chọn.
        private string _selectedAvailability = "Tất cả trạng thái";

        public string SelectedAvailability
        {
            get => _selectedAvailability;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedAvailability != value)
                {
                    _selectedAvailability = value ?? "Tất cả trạng thái";
                    OnPropertyChanged();

                    ApplyFilters();
                }
            }
        }

        // Món đang được chọn trên DataGrid.
        private Product? _selectedProduct;

        public Product? SelectedProduct
        {
            get => _selectedProduct;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedProduct != value)
                {
                    _selectedProduct = value;
                    OnPropertyChanged();

                    EditCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
                    DeleteCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
                    ToggleAvailabilityCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
                }
            }
        }

        // Các command liên kết với nút trên giao diện.
        public AsyncRelayCommand AddCommand { get; }
        public AsyncRelayCommand EditCommand { get; }
        public AsyncRelayCommand DeleteCommand { get; }
        public AsyncRelayCommand SearchCommand { get; }
        public AsyncRelayCommand ToggleAvailabilityCommand { get; }

        // Khởi tạo ViewModel và các command.
        public MenuViewModel(
            MenuService menuService,
            IMenuDialogService dialogService)
        {
            _menuService = menuService;
            _dialogService = dialogService;

            AddCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(
                AddProductCommandAsync,
                onError: HandleUnexpectedError);

            EditCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(
                EditProductAsync,
                () => SelectedProduct != null,
                HandleUnexpectedError);

            DeleteCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(
                DeleteProductAsync,
                () => SelectedProduct != null,
                HandleUnexpectedError);

            SearchCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(
                SearchProductsAsync,
                onError: HandleUnexpectedError);

            ToggleAvailabilityCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(
                ToggleAvailabilityAsync,
                () => SelectedProduct != null,
                HandleUnexpectedError);
        }

        // Tải danh sách món và danh mục từ database.
        public async Task LoadProductsAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                _allProducts = await _menuService.GetAllProductsAsync();

                // Sắp xếp mặc định theo ProductId tăng dần.
                _allProducts = _allProducts
                    .OrderBy(p => p.ProductId)
                    .ToList();

                // Cập nhật danh sách danh mục.
                Categories.Clear();
                Categories.Add("Tất cả");

                foreach (string category in _allProducts
                    .Where(p => p.Category != null)
                    .Select(p => p.Category.CategoryName)
                    .Distinct()
                    .OrderBy(c => c))
                {
                    Categories.Add(category);
                }

                // Nếu danh mục cũ không còn tồn tại,
                // chuyển về "Tất cả".
                if (!Categories.Contains(SelectedCategory))
                {
                    _selectedCategory = "Tất cả";
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedCategory));
                }

                ApplyFilters();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Không thể tải thực đơn: " + ex.Message,
                    "Lỗi",
                    DialogMessageType.Error);
            }
        }

        // Tìm kiếm món khi nhấn nút Tìm món.
        private Task SearchProductsAsync()
        {
            ApplyFilters();
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        }

        // Áp dụng tìm kiếm, lọc và sắp xếp.
        private void ApplyFilters()
        {
            string keyword = SearchKeyword.Trim();

            IEnumerable<Product> result = _allProducts;

            // 1. Tìm kiếm theo tên món.
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(keyword))
            {
                result = result.Where(p =>
                    (p.ProductName ?? "").IndexOf(
                        keyword,
                        StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0);
            }

            // 2. Lọc theo danh mục.
            if (SelectedCategory != "Tất cả")
            {
                result = result.Where(p =>
                    p.Category != null &&
                    p.Category.CategoryName == SelectedCategory);
            }

            // 3. Lọc theo trạng thái bán.
            if (SelectedAvailability == "Đang bán")
            {
                result = result.Where(p => p.IsAvailable);
            }
            else if (SelectedAvailability == "Ngừng bán")
            {
                result = result.Where(p => !p.IsAvailable);
            }

            // 4. Sắp xếp theo giá thấp nhất của các size đang bán.
            if (SelectedSort == "Giá thấp đến cao")
            {
                result = result
                    .OrderBy(GetLowestPrice)
                    .ThenBy(p => p.ProductId);
            }
            else if (SelectedSort == "Giá cao đến thấp")
            {
                result = result
                    .OrderByDescending(GetLowestPrice)
                    .ThenBy(p => p.ProductId);
            }
            else
            {
                // Mặc định sắp xếp theo ID tăng dần.
                result = result.OrderBy(p => p.ProductId);
            }

            // Cập nhật danh sách hiển thị.
            Products.Clear();

            foreach (Product product in result.ToList())
            {
                Products.Add(product);
            }

            // Bỏ chọn món cũ sau khi danh sách thay đổi.
            SelectedProduct = null;
        }

        // Lấy giá thấp nhất trong các size đang được bán.
        private static decimal GetLowestPrice(Product product)
        {
            var prices = product.ProductSizes
                .Where(s => s.IsAvailable)
                .Select(s => s.Price)
                .ToList();

            return prices.Count > 0
                ? prices.Min()
                : decimal.MaxValue;
        }

        // Thêm món mới.
        private async Task AddProductCommandAsync()
        {
            Product? product = _dialogService.ShowProductDialog();

            if (product == null)
                return;

            try
            {
                await _menuService.AddProductAsync(product);

                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Thêm món uống thành công!",
                    "Thông báo",
                    DialogMessageType.Information);

                await LoadProductsAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Không thể thêm món: " + ex.Message,
                    "Lỗi",
                    DialogMessageType.Error);
            }
        }

        // Sửa món đang được chọn.
        private async Task EditProductAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedProduct == null)
                return;

            int productId = SelectedProduct.ProductId;

            // ProductDialog tạo đối tượng mới,
            // không sửa trực tiếp đối tượng đang hiển thị.
            Product? product =
                _dialogService.ShowProductDialog(SelectedProduct);

            if (product == null)
                return;

            product.ProductId = productId;

            try
            {
                await _menuService.UpdateProductAsync(product);

                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Cập nhật món uống thành công!",
                    "Thông báo",
                    DialogMessageType.Information);

                await LoadProductsAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Không thể sửa món: " + ex.Message,
                    "Lỗi",
                    DialogMessageType.Error);
            }
        }

        // Bật bán hoặc ngừng bán món.
        private async Task ToggleAvailabilityAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedProduct == null)
                return;

            Product product = SelectedProduct;

            bool oldStatus = product.IsAvailable;
            bool newStatus = !oldStatus;

            try
            {
                product.IsAvailable = newStatus;

                await _menuService.UpdateProductAsync(product);

                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    newStatus
                        ? "Đã bật bán món uống."
                        : "Đã ngừng bán món uống.",
                    "Thông báo",
                    DialogMessageType.Information);

                await LoadProductsAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Khôi phục trạng thái nếu cập nhật thất bại.
                product.IsAvailable = oldStatus;

                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Không thể cập nhật trạng thái bán: " + ex.Message,
                    "Lỗi",
                    DialogMessageType.Error);

                await LoadProductsAsync();
            }
        }

        // Ngừng bán món sau khi người dùng xác nhận.
        private async Task DeleteProductAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedProduct == null)
                return;

            Product product = SelectedProduct;

            if (!_dialogService.ConfirmDelete(product.ProductName))
                return;

            try
            {
                await _menuService.DeleteProductAsync(product.ProductId);

                SelectedProduct = null;

                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Đã ngừng bán món uống thành công.",
                    "Thông báo",
                    DialogMessageType.Information);

                await LoadProductsAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                    "Không thể ngừng bán món:\n" + ex.Message,
                    "Lỗi",
                    DialogMessageType.Error);
            }
        }

        // Xử lý lỗi bất ngờ từ AsyncRelayCommand.
        private void HandleUnexpectedError(Exception ex)
        {
            _dialogService.ShowMessage(
                "Đã xảy ra lỗi khi thực hiện thao tác:\n" + ex.Message,
                "Lỗi",
                DialogMessageType.Error);
        }

        // Thông báo cho giao diện khi thuộc tính thay đổi.
        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        protected void OnPropertyChanged(
            [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }
}using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class MainWindowViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly NavigationService _navigationService;
        private readonly WindowService _windowService;

        private string _currentPage = string.Empty;

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        public string CurrentPage
        {
            get => _currentPage;
            private set
            {
                if (_currentPage == value)
                {
                    return;
                }

                _currentPage = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        public Visibility HomeVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Home") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility MenuVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Menu") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility ProductVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Product") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility OrdersVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Orders") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility KdsVisibility =>
            CanAccess("KDS") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility InventoryVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Inventory") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility RecipeManagementVisibility =>
            UserSession.Current?.RoleName == "Admin"
                ? Visibility.Visible
                : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility ReportVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Report") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility TableVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Table") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public Visibility EmployeeVisibility =>
            CanAccess("Employee") ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        public ICommand NavigateHomeCommand { get; }
        public ICommand NavigateMenuCommand { get; }
        public ICommand NavigateProductCommand { get; }
        public ICommand NavigateOrdersCommand { get; }
        public ICommand NavigateKdsCommand { get; }
        public ICommand InventoryCommand { get; }
        public ICommand RecipeManagementCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ReportCommand { get; }
        public ICommand TableCommand { get; }
        public ICommand EmployeeCommand { get; }
        public ICommand LogoutCommand { get; }

        public string CurrentUserName =>
            UserSession.Current?.FullName ?? "Chưa đăng nhập";

        public string CurrentRoleName =>
            UserSession.Current?.RoleName ?? string.Empty;

        public MainWindowViewModel(
            NavigationService navigationService,
            WindowService windowService)
        {
            _navigationService = navigationService;
            _windowService = windowService;

            _navigationService.Navigated += OnNavigated;

            NavigateHomeCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Home"));

            NavigateMenuCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Menu"));

            NavigateProductCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Product"));

            NavigateOrdersCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Orders"));

            NavigateKdsCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("KDS"));

            InventoryCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Inventory"));

            RecipeManagementCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("RecipeManagement"));

            ReportCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Report"));

            TableCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Table"));

            EmployeeCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => Navigate("Employee"));

            LogoutCommand = new RelayCommand(
                () => _windowService.Logout());
        }

        private void OnNavigated(string pageName)
        {
            CurrentPage = pageName;
        }

        private bool CanAccess(string screen)
        {
            return UserSession.Current?.CanAccess(screen) == true;
        }

        private void Navigate(string screen)
        {
            if (!CanAccess(screen))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    "Tài khoản của bạn không có quyền truy cập chức năng này.",
                    "Không đủ quyền",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK,
                    MessageBoxImage.Warning);

                return;
            }

            _navigationService.Navigate(screen);
        }

        private void OnPropertyChanged(
            [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }
}
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class LowStockAlertViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly LowStockAlertService _service;

        public ObservableCollection<LowStockAlertItem> Alerts
        { get; } = new();

        public int TotalAlerts => Alerts.Count;

        public int OutOfStockCount =>
            Alerts.Count(x => x.CurrentStock == 0);

        public int LowStockCount =>
            Alerts.Count(x => x.CurrentStock > 0);

        private bool _isLoading;

        public bool IsLoading
        {
            get => _isLoading;
            private set
            {
                if (_isLoading == value)
                    return;

                _isLoading = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _statusMessage = "";

        public string StatusMessage
        {
            get => _statusMessage;
            private set
            {
                _statusMessage = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        public ICommand RefreshCommand { get; }

        public LowStockAlertViewModel(
            LowStockAlertService service)
        {
            _service = service;
            RefreshCommand = new RefreshLowStockCommand(this);
        }

        public async Task LoadAsync()
        {
            if (IsLoading)
                return;

            IsLoading = true;
            StatusMessage = "Đang tải dữ liệu tồn kho...";

            try
            {
                var results = await _service.GetAlertsAsync();

                Alerts.Clear();

                foreach (var item in results)
                {
                    Alerts.Add(item);
                }

                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TotalAlerts));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(OutOfStockCount));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LowStockCount));

                StatusMessage = Alerts.Count == 0
                    ? "Không có nguyên liệu nào cần cảnh báo."
                    : $"Đã cập nhật {DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss}. " +
                      $"Có {TotalAlerts} nguyên liệu cần chú ý.";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage =
                    $"Không thể tải cảnh báo: {ex.Message}";
            }
            finally
            {
                IsLoading = false;
            }
        }

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        private void OnPropertyChanged(
            [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }

        private sealed class RefreshLowStockCommand : ICommand
        {
            private readonly LowStockAlertViewModel _viewModel;

            public RefreshLowStockCommand(
                LowStockAlertViewModel viewModel)
            {
                _viewModel = viewModel;

                _viewModel.PropertyChanged += (_, args) =>
                {
                    if (args.PropertyName == nameof(IsLoading))
                    {
                        CanExecuteChanged?.Invoke(
                            this, EventArgs.Empty);
                    }
                };
            }

            public event EventHandler? CanExecuteChanged;

            public bool CanExecute(object? parameter)
            {
                return !_viewModel.IsLoading;
            }

            public async void Execute(object? parameter)
            {
                if (CanExecute(parameter))
                {
                    await _viewModel.LoadAsync();
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class LoginViewModel : BaseViewModel
    {
        private readonly AuthenticationService _authenticationService;
        private readonly IMessageDialogService _dialogService;
        private readonly WindowService _windowService;

        private string _username = string.Empty;
        private string _password = string.Empty;

        public string Username
        {
            get => _username;
            set => SetProperty(ref _username, value);
        }

        public string Password
        {
            get => _password;
            set => SetProperty(ref _password, value);
        }

        public ICommand LoginCommand { get; }

        public LoginViewModel(
            AuthenticationService authenticationService,
            IMessageDialogService dialogService,
            WindowService windowService)
        {
            _authenticationService = authenticationService;
            _dialogService = dialogService;
            _windowService = windowService;

            LoginCommand = new RelayCommand(Login);
        }

        private void Login()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Username))
            {
                _dialogService.ShowWarning(
                    "Thông báo",
                    "Vui lòng nhập tên đăng nhập!");

                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Password))
            {
                _dialogService.ShowWarning(
                    "Thông báo",
                    "Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu!");

                return;
            }

            try
            {
                var user = _authenticationService.Authenticate(
                    Username.Trim(),
                    Password);

                if (user == null)
                {
                    _dialogService.ShowWarning(
                        "Đăng nhập thất bại",
                        "Tên đăng nhập hoặc mật khẩu không đúng.");

                    return;
                }

                UserSession.Start(
                    user.EmployeeId,
                    user.RoleId,
                    user.Username,
                    user.FullName,
                    user.RoleName);

                _windowService.OpenMainWindowAfterLogin();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _dialogService.ShowError(
                    "Lỗi đăng nhập",
                    ex.Message);
            }
        }
    }
}
using CoffeeRuu.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class KdsViewModel : NotifyBase
    {
        private readonly OrderService _service;
        private readonly DispatcherTimer _refreshTimer;
        private bool _refreshInProgress;

        public ObservableCollection<KdsItemRow> Items { get; } = new();

        public ObservableCollection<EmployeeChoice> Employees { get; } = new();

        public ICollectionView FilteredItems { get; }

        public string[] StatusOptions { get; } =
        {
            "Tất cả",
            "Queued",
            "Preparing"
        };

        private EmployeeChoice? _selectedEmployee;

        public EmployeeChoice? SelectedEmployee
        {
            get => _selectedEmployee;
            set => Set(ref _selectedEmployee, value);
        }

        private KdsItemRow? _selectedItem;

        public KdsItemRow? SelectedItem
        {
            get => _selectedItem;
            set
            {
                if (Set(ref _selectedItem, value))
                {
                    Notify(nameof(CanStart));
                    Notify(nameof(CanReady));
                }
            }
        }

        private string _searchKds = "";

        public string SearchKds
        {
            get => _searchKds;
            set
            {
                if (Set(ref _searchKds, value))
                    RefreshFilter();
            }
        }

        private string _filterStatus = "Tất cả";

        public string FilterStatus
        {
            get => _filterStatus;
            set
            {
                if (Set(ref _filterStatus, value))
                    RefreshFilter();
            }
        }

        private string _message = "";

        public string Message
        {
            get => _message;
            set => Set(ref _message, value);
        }

        private bool _isBusy;

        public bool IsBusy
        {
            get => _isBusy;
            private set
            {
                if (Set(ref _isBusy, value))
                {
                    Notify(nameof(CanStart));
                    Notify(nameof(CanReady));
                }
            }
        }

        public string CurrentEmployeeDisplay
        {
            get
            {
                var session = UserSession.Current;

                if (session == null)
                    return "Chưa đăng nhập";

                return $"{session.EmployeeId} - " +
                       $"{session.FullName} " +
                       $"({session.RoleName})";
            }
        }

        public bool CanOperateKds
        {
            get
            {
                var session = UserSession.Current;

                return session != null &&
                       session.EmployeeId > 0 &&
                       string.Equals(
                           session.RoleName.Trim(),
                           "Barista",
                           StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            }
        }

        public string KdsPermissionMessage =>
            CanOperateKds
                ? "Barista: có quyền cập nhật pha chế."
                : "Chế độ chỉ xem - chỉ Barista được cập nhật pha chế.";

        public bool CanStart =>
            !IsBusy &&
            CanOperateKds &&
            SelectedItem?.PreparationStatus == "Queued";

        public bool CanReady =>
            !IsBusy &&
            CanOperateKds &&
            SelectedItem?.PreparationStatus == "Preparing";

        public ICommand RefreshCommand { get; }
        public ICommand StartCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ReadyCommand { get; }

        public KdsViewModel(OrderService service)
        {
            _service = service ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(service));

            FilteredItems =
                CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(Items);

            FilteredItems.Filter = FilterItem;

            FilteredItems.GroupDescriptions?.Add(
                new PropertyGroupDescription(
                    nameof(KdsItemRow.OrderCode)));

            RefreshCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteAsync(LoadAsync));

            StartCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteAsync(
                    () => ChangeStatusAsync("Preparing")));

            ReadyCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteAsync(
                    () => ChangeStatusAsync("Ready")));

            _refreshTimer = new DispatcherTimer
            {
                Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4)
            };

            _refreshTimer.Tick += RefreshTimer_Tick;
        }

        public Task InitializeAsync()
        {
            return ExecuteAsync(LoadAsync);
        }

        public void StartAutoRefresh()
        {
            if (!_refreshTimer.IsEnabled)
                _refreshTimer.Start();
        }

        public void StopAutoRefresh()
        {
            _refreshTimer.Stop();
        }

        private async void RefreshTimer_Tick(
            object? sender,
            EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_refreshInProgress || IsBusy)
                return;

            _refreshInProgress = true;

            try
            {
                await LoadAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Message = "Không thể làm mới KDS: " + ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                _refreshInProgress = false;
            }
        }

        private bool FilterItem(object obj)
        {
            if (obj is not KdsItemRow item)
                return false;

            if (item.PreparationStatus != "Queued" &&
                item.PreparationStatus != "Preparing")
            {
                return false;
            }

            string keyword = SearchKds.Trim();

            bool matchSearch =
                keyword.Length == 0 ||
                (item.OrderCode ?? "").Contains(
                    keyword,
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                (item.ProductName ?? "").Contains(
                    keyword,
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            bool matchStatus =
                FilterStatus == "Tất cả" ||
                item.PreparationStatus == FilterStatus;

            return matchSearch && matchStatus;
        }

        private void RefreshFilter()
        {
            FilteredItems.Refresh();

            if (SelectedItem != null &&
                !FilterItem(SelectedItem))
            {
                SelectedItem = null;
            }
        }

        private async Task LoadAsync()
        {
            int? selectedEmployeeId =
                SelectedEmployee?.EmployeeId;

            int? selectedItemId =
                SelectedItem?.OrderItemId;

            var employees =
                await _service.GetEmployeesAsync();

            var items =
                await _service.GetKdsAsync();

            var baristas = employees
                .Where(e => string.Equals(
                    e.RoleName.Trim(),
                    "Barista",
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                .ToList();

            var activeItems = items
                .Where(i =>
                    i.PreparationStatus == "Queued" ||
                    i.PreparationStatus == "Preparing")
                .ToList();

            bool employeesChanged =
                Employees.Count != baristas.Count ||
                Employees.Zip(
                    baristas,
                    (a, b) =>
                        a.EmployeeId == b.EmployeeId &&
                        a.FullName == b.FullName &&
                        a.RoleName == b.RoleName)
                .Any(same => !same);

            if (employeesChanged)
            {
                Employees.Clear();

                foreach (var employee in baristas)
                {
                    Employees.Add(employee);
                }

                int? employeeIdToShow =
                    selectedEmployeeId ??
                    UserSession.Current?.EmployeeId;

                SelectedEmployee =
                    Employees.FirstOrDefault(e =>
                        e.EmployeeId == employeeIdToShow)
                    ?? Employees.FirstOrDefault();
            }

            bool itemsChanged =
                Items.Count != activeItems.Count ||
                Items.Zip(
                    activeItems,
                    (a, b) =>
                        a.OrderItemId == b.OrderItemId &&
                        a.OrderCode == b.OrderCode &&
                        a.ProductName == b.ProductName &&
                        a.SizeName == b.SizeName &&
                        a.Quantity == b.Quantity &&
                        a.Note == b.Note &&
                        a.PreparationStatus == b.PreparationStatus)
                .Any(same => !same);

            if (itemsChanged)
            {
                Items.Clear();

                foreach (var item in activeItems)
                {
                    Items.Add(item);
                }

                SelectedItem =
                    Items.FirstOrDefault(i =>
                        i.OrderItemId == selectedItemId &&
                        FilterItem(i));

                FilteredItems.Refresh();
            }

            Notify(nameof(CurrentEmployeeDisplay));
            Notify(nameof(CanOperateKds));
            Notify(nameof(KdsPermissionMessage));
            Notify(nameof(CanStart));
            Notify(nameof(CanReady));
        }

        private async Task ChangeStatusAsync(string nextStatus)
        {
            if (!CanOperateKds ||
                UserSession.Current == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Chỉ tài khoản Barista được cập nhật pha chế.");
            }

            if (SelectedItem == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Hãy chọn món cần xử lý.");
            }

            bool valid =
                (SelectedItem.PreparationStatus == "Queued" &&
                 nextStatus == "Preparing") ||
                (SelectedItem.PreparationStatus == "Preparing" &&
                 nextStatus == "Ready");

            if (!valid)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Chuyển trạng thái pha chế không hợp lệ.");
            }

            int itemId = SelectedItem.OrderItemId;
            int employeeId = UserSession.Current.EmployeeId;

            await _service.UpdatePreparationAsync(
                itemId,
                nextStatus,
                employeeId);

            await LoadAsync();

            Message = nextStatus == "Preparing"
                ? "Đã bắt đầu pha chế."
                : "Món đã Ready và được ẩn khỏi danh sách đang pha.";
        }

        private async Task ExecuteAsync(Func<Task> action)
        {
            if (IsBusy)
                return;

            IsBusy = true;
            Message = "";

            try
            {
                await action();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Message = "Lỗi: " + ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                IsBusy = false;
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Data;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class IngredientViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly InventoryService _inventoryService;

        // Danh sách nguyên liệu
        public ObservableCollection<Ingredient> Ingredients
        { get; } = new();

        // Danh sách hiển thị sau khi lọc
        public ICollectionView IngredientsView { get; }

        // Nguyên liệu được chọn
        private Ingredient? _selectedIngredient;

        public Ingredient? SelectedIngredient
        {
            get => _selectedIngredient;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedIngredient == value)
                    return;

                _selectedIngredient = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();

                if (value != null)
                {
                    IngredientNameInput = value.IngredientName;
                    UnitInput = value.Unit;
                    MinimumStockText =
                        value.MinimumStock.ToString(
                            CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
                }
                else
                {
                    ClearInputs();
                }

                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanEditUnit));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ToggleButtonText));
                UpdateCommands();
            }
        }

        // Form nhập liệu
        private string _ingredientNameInput = "";

        public string IngredientNameInput
        {
            get => _ingredientNameInput;
            set
            {
                _ingredientNameInput = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _unitInput = "";

        public string UnitInput
        {
            get => _unitInput;
            set
            {
                _unitInput = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _minimumStockText = "0";

        public string MinimumStockText
        {
            get => _minimumStockText;
            set
            {
                _minimumStockText = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        // Tìm kiếm nguyên liệu
        private string _searchText = "";

        public string SearchText
        {
            get => _searchText;
            set
            {
                _searchText = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                IngredientsView.Refresh();
            }
        }

        // Trạng thái xử lý
        private bool _isBusy;

        public bool IsBusy
        {
            get => _isBusy;
            private set
            {
                _isBusy = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                UpdateCommands();
            }
        }

        private string _statusMessage = "";

        public string StatusMessage
        {
            get => _statusMessage;
            set
            {
                _statusMessage = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        public bool CanEditUnit => SelectedIngredient == null;

        public string ToggleButtonText =>
            SelectedIngredient?.IsActive == true
                ? "Ngừng sử dụng"
                : "Kích hoạt lại";

        // Commands
        public RelayCommand LoadCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand AddCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand UpdateCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand ToggleActiveCommand { get; }
        public RelayCommand ClearCommand { get; }

        // Constructor
        public IngredientViewModel(
            InventoryService inventoryService)
        {
            _inventoryService = inventoryService;

            IngredientsView =
                CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(Ingredients);

            IngredientsView.Filter = FilterIngredient;

            LoadCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    LoadIngredientsAsync),
                () => !IsBusy);

            AddCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    AddIngredientAsync),
                () => !IsBusy && SelectedIngredient == null);

            UpdateCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    UpdateIngredientAsync),
                () => !IsBusy && SelectedIngredient != null);

            ToggleActiveCommand = new RelayCommand(
                async () => await ExecuteSafeAsync(
                    ToggleActiveAsync),
                () => !IsBusy && SelectedIngredient != null);

            ClearCommand = new RelayCommand(
                ClearForm,
                () => !IsBusy);

            // Tải danh sách khi ViewModel được tạo
            _ = ExecuteSafeAsync(LoadIngredientsAsync);
        }

        // 1. Lấy danh sách nguyên liệu
        private async Task LoadIngredientsAsync()
        {
            int? selectedId = SelectedIngredient?.IngredientId;

            var items = await _inventoryService
                .GetIngredientsAsync();

            Ingredients.Clear();

            foreach (var item in items)
            {
                Ingredients.Add(item);
            }

            SelectedIngredient = selectedId.HasValue
                ? Ingredients.FirstOrDefault(
                    i => i.IngredientId == selectedId.Value)
                : null;

            IngredientsView.Refresh();
        }

        // 2. Thêm nguyên liệu
        private async Task AddIngredientAsync()
        {
            decimal minimumStock = ParseMinimumStock();

            await _inventoryService.AddIngredientAsync(
                IngredientNameInput,
                UnitInput,
                minimumStock);

            await LoadIngredientsAsync();

            ClearForm();

            StatusMessage = "Thêm nguyên liệu thành công.";
        }

        // 3. Sửa nguyên liệu
        private async Task UpdateIngredientAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedIngredient == null)
                return;

            decimal minimumStock = ParseMinimumStock();

            int id = SelectedIngredient.IngredientId;

            // Giữ nguyên đơn vị của nguyên liệu đã có
            await _inventoryService.UpdateIngredientAsync(
                id,
                IngredientNameInput,
                SelectedIngredient.Unit,
                minimumStock);

            await LoadIngredientsAsync();

            StatusMessage = "Cập nhật nguyên liệu thành công.";
        }

        // 4. Thay đổi trạng thái nguyên liệu
        private async Task ToggleActiveAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedIngredient == null)
                return;

            bool newStatus = !SelectedIngredient.IsActive;

            await _inventoryService.SetIngredientActiveAsync(
                SelectedIngredient.IngredientId,
                newStatus);

            await LoadIngredientsAsync();

            StatusMessage = newStatus
                ? "Đã kích hoạt nguyên liệu."
                : "Đã ngừng sử dụng nguyên liệu.";
        }

        // 5. Lọc nguyên liệu theo tên
        private bool FilterIngredient(object obj)
        {
            if (obj is not Ingredient ingredient)
                return false;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SearchText))
                return true;

            return ingredient.IngredientName.Contains(
                SearchText.Trim(),
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        }

        // 6. Chuyển ngưỡng tồn kho sang decimal
        private decimal ParseMinimumStock()
        {
            if (!decimal.TryParse(
                MinimumStockText,
                NumberStyles.Number,
                CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
                out decimal result))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Ngưỡng tồn kho phải là một số hợp lệ.");
            }

            return result;
        }

        // 7. Xóa form
        private void ClearInputs()
        {
            IngredientNameInput = "";
            UnitInput = "";
            MinimumStockText = "0";
        }

        private void ClearForm()
        {
            SelectedIngredient = null;
            ClearInputs();
        }

        // 8. Xử lý các thao tác bất đồng bộ
        private async Task ExecuteSafeAsync(Func<Task> action)
        {
            if (IsBusy)
                return;

            try
            {
                IsBusy = true;
                StatusMessage = "";

                await action();
            }
            catch (ArgumentException ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = ex.Message;
            }
            catch (InvalidOperationException ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = ex.Message;
            }
            catch (KeyNotFoundException ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = ex.Message;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = "Có lỗi xảy ra: " + ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                IsBusy = false;
            }
        }

        // 9. Cập nhật trạng thái Command
        private void UpdateCommands()
        {
            LoadCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            AddCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            UpdateCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            ToggleActiveCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
            ClearCommand?.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
        }

        // 10. Thông báo thay đổi Property
        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler?
            PropertyChanged;

        private void OnPropertyChanged(
            [CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }
}
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class HomeViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly TableService _tableService;

        public ObservableCollection<CafeTable> Tables { get; } = new();

        public HomeViewModel(TableService tableService)
        {
            _tableService = tableService;
        }

        public async Task LoadTablesAsync()
        {
            var items = await _tableService.GetTablesAsync();
            Tables.Clear();
            foreach (var item in items)
            {
                Tables.Add(item);
            }
        }

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;
        protected void OnPropertyChanged([CallerMemberName] string? propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }
}
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using CoffeeRuu.Services;

namespace CoffeeRuu.ViewModels
{
    public class EmployeeManagementViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly EmployeeService _employeeService;

        public ObservableCollection<Employee> Employees { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<Employee> FilteredEmployees { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<Role> Roles { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<Role> RoleOptions { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<RoleFilterOption> RoleFilters { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<RoleStatistic> RoleStatistics { get; } = new();

        public ObservableCollection<string> StatusFilters { get; } = new()
        {
            "Tất cả trạng thái",
            "Đang hoạt động",
            "Ngừng hoạt động"
        };

        private Employee? _selectedEmployee;

        public Employee? SelectedEmployee
        {
            get => _selectedEmployee;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedEmployee == value) return;

                _selectedEmployee = value;

                OnPropertyChanged();
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanUpdate));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanDeactivate));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsSelectedAdmin));
            }
        }

        private string _username = "";
        public string Username
        {
            get => _username;
            set
            {
                _username = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _fullName = "";
        public string FullName
        {
            get => _fullName;
            set
            {
                _fullName = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _phone = "";
        public string Phone
        {
            get => _phone;
            set
            {
                _phone = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _email = "";
        public string Email
        {
            get => _email;
            set
            {
                _email = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private string _password = "";
        public string Password
        {
            get => _password;
            set
            {
                _password = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private bool _isActive = true;
        public bool IsActive
        {
            get => _isActive;
            set
            {
                _isActive = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private Role? _selectedRole;
        public Role? SelectedRole
        {
            get => _selectedRole;
            set
            {
                _selectedRole = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private bool _isFormVisible;

        public bool IsFormVisible
        {
            get => _isFormVisible;
            private set
            {
                _isFormVisible = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FormVisibility));
            }
        }

        public Visibility FormVisibility =>
            IsFormVisible ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

        private bool _isAddMode;

        public bool IsAddMode
        {
            get => _isAddMode;
            private set
            {
                _isAddMode = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FormTitle));
            }
        }

        public string FormTitle =>
            IsAddMode ? "Thêm nhân viên" : "Chỉnh sửa nhân viên";

        private string _searchText = "";

        public string SearchText
        {
            get => _searchText;
            set
            {
                _searchText = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                ApplyFilters();
            }
        }

        private RoleFilterOption? _selectedRoleFilter;

        public RoleFilterOption? SelectedRoleFilter
        {
            get => _selectedRoleFilter;
            set
            {
                _selectedRoleFilter = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                ApplyFilters();
            }
        }

        private string _selectedStatusFilter = "Tất cả trạng thái";

        public string SelectedStatusFilter
        {
            get => _selectedStatusFilter;
            set
            {
                _selectedStatusFilter = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
                ApplyFilters();
            }
        }

        private string _statusMessage = "";

        public string StatusMessage
        {
            get => _statusMessage;
            set
            {
                _statusMessage = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private int _totalCount;

        public int TotalCount
        {
            get => _totalCount;
            private set
            {
                _totalCount = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private int _activeCount;

        public int ActiveCount
        {
            get => _activeCount;
            private set
            {
                _activeCount = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        private int _inactiveCount;

        public int InactiveCount
        {
            get => _inactiveCount;
            private set
            {
                _inactiveCount = value;
                OnPropertyChanged();
            }
        }

        public int FilteredCount => FilteredEmployees.Count;

        public bool CanUpdate => SelectedEmployee != null;

        public bool IsSelectedAdmin =>
            IsAdminRole(SelectedEmployee?.Role?.RoleName);

        public bool CanDeactivate =>
            SelectedEmployee != null &&
            SelectedEmployee.IsActive &&
            !IsSelectedAdmin;

        public ICommand LoadCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenAddCommand { get; }
        public ICommand AddCommand { get; }
        public ICommand EditEmployeeCommand { get; }
        public ICommand UpdateCommand { get; }
        public ICommand DeactivateCommand { get; }
        public ICommand CancelCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ClearCommand { get; }

        public EmployeeManagementViewModel(EmployeeService employeeService)
        {
            _employeeService = employeeService;

            LoadCommand = new RelayCommand(LoadData);
            OpenAddCommand = new RelayCommand(OpenAddForm);
            AddCommand = new RelayCommand(AddEmployee);
            EditEmployeeCommand = new ParameterCommand(EditEmployee);
            UpdateCommand = new RelayCommand(UpdateEmployee);
            DeactivateCommand = new RelayCommand(DeactivateEmployee);
            CancelCommand = new RelayCommand(CancelForm);
            ClearCommand = new RelayCommand(CancelForm);

            LoadData();
        }

        private static bool IsAdminRole(string? roleName) =>
            string.Equals(
                roleName?.Trim(),
                "Admin",
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        private Role? FindRole(int roleId) =>
            Roles.FirstOrDefault(r => r.RoleId == roleId);

        private void OpenAddForm()
        {
            ResetFormFields();
            SelectedEmployee = null;
            IsAddMode = true;
            IsFormVisible = true;
            StatusMessage = "";
        }

        private void EditEmployee(object? parameter)
        {
            if (parameter is not Employee employee) return;

            SelectedEmployee = employee;

            Username = employee.Username;
            FullName = employee.FullName;
            Phone = employee.Phone ?? "";
            Email = employee.Email ?? "";
            IsActive = employee.IsActive;
            SelectedRole = FindRole(employee.RoleId);

            // De trong de khong thay doi mat khau cu.
            Password = "";

            IsAddMode = false;
            IsFormVisible = true;
            StatusMessage = "";
        }

        private void LoadData()
        {
            try
            {
                var roles = _employeeService.GetRoles();
                var employees = _employeeService.GetAll();

                Roles.Clear();
                RoleOptions.Clear();
                RoleFilters.Clear();

                foreach (var role in roles)
                {
                    Roles.Add(role);

                    if (!IsAdminRole(role.RoleName))
                    {
                        RoleOptions.Add(role);
                    }

                    RoleFilters.Add(
                        new RoleFilterOption(role.RoleId, role.RoleName));
                }

                RoleFilters.Insert(
                    0,
                    new RoleFilterOption(null, "Tất cả vai trò"));

                if (_selectedRoleFilter == null ||
                    !RoleFilters.Any(r =>
                        r.RoleId == _selectedRoleFilter.RoleId))
                {
                    SelectedRoleFilter = RoleFilters.FirstOrDefault();
                }

                Employees.Clear();

                foreach (var employee in employees)
                {
                    Employees.Add(employee);
                }

                UpdateStatistics();
                ApplyFilters();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = "Không thể tải dữ liệu: " + ex.Message;

                MessageBox.Show(
                    StatusMessage,
                    "Lỗi",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK,
                    MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }

        private void UpdateStatistics()
        {
            TotalCount = Employees.Count;
            ActiveCount = Employees.Count(e => e.IsActive);
            InactiveCount = Employees.Count(e => !e.IsActive);

            RoleStatistics.Clear();

            foreach (var role in Roles)
            {
                var roleEmployees = Employees
                    .Where(e => e.RoleId == role.RoleId)
                    .ToList();

                RoleStatistics.Add(new RoleStatistic
                {
                    RoleName = role.RoleName,
                    TotalCount = roleEmployees.Count,
                    ActiveCount = roleEmployees.Count(e => e.IsActive),
                    InactiveCount = roleEmployees.Count(e => !e.IsActive)
                });
            }
        }

        private void ApplyFilters()
        {
